                    requested_level <= requested_level == 4 ? 3'd0 : requested_level + 1'b1;
            end else debounce_count <= debounce_count + 1'b1;
            // A press accepted on this same edge takes effect next frame.
            if (x == 0 && y == 0) level <= requested_level;
        end
    end

    function [7:0] contrast8;
        input [7:0] value;
        input [2:0] setting;
        reg signed [12:0] centered, numerator, scaled;
        begin
            centered = $signed({1'b0,value}) - 13'sd128;
            // floor(((value-128)*gain_quarters + 512 + 2)/4).
            // Signed shifts/adds implement gain_quarters 4,5,6,7,8; the
            // arithmetic >>> is floor division even below zero, before clip.
            case (setting)
                0: numerator = (centered <<< 2) + 13'sd514;
                1: numerator = (centered <<< 2) + centered + 13'sd514;
                2: numerator = (centered <<< 2) + (centered <<< 1) + 13'sd514;
                3: numerator = (centered <<< 3) - centered + 13'sd514;
                default: numerator = (centered <<< 3) + 13'sd514;
            endcase
            scaled = numerator >>> 2;
            if (setting == 0) contrast8 = value;
            else if (scaled < 0) contrast8 = 0;
            else if (scaled > 255) contrast8 = 255;
            else contrast8 = scaled[7:0];
        end
    endfunction

    // The same 5x7 glyph convention as style_color_probe_overlay, scaled 2x.
    function [34:0] glyph;
        input [7:0] c;
        begin
            case(c)
                "A":glyph=35'b01110_10001_10001_11111_10001_10001_10001;
                "B":glyph=35'b11110_10001_10001_11110_10001_10001_11110;
                "C":glyph=35'b01111_10000_10000_10000_10000_10000_01111;
                "D":glyph=35'b11110_10001_10001_10001_10001_10001_11110;
                "E":glyph=35'b11111_10000_10000_11110_10000_10000_11111;
                "F":glyph=35'b11111_10000_10000_11110_10000_10000_10000;
                "G":glyph=35'b01111_10000_10000_10111_10001_10001_01111;
                "H":glyph=35'b10001_10001_10001_11111_10001_10001_10001;
                "I":glyph=35'b11111_00100_00100_00100_00100_00100_11111;
                "K":glyph=35'b10001_10010_10100_11000_10100_10010_10001;
                "L":glyph=35'b10000_10000_10000_10000_10000_10000_11111;
                "N":glyph=35'b10001_11001_10101_10011_10001_10001_10001;
                "O":glyph=35'b01110_10001_10001_10001_10001_10001_01110;
                "P":glyph=35'b11110_10001_10001_11110_10000_10000_10000;
                "R":glyph=35'b11110_10001_10001_11110_10100_10010_10001;
                "S":glyph=35'b01111_10000_10000_01110_00001_00001_11110;
                "T":glyph=35'b11111_00100_00100_00100_00100_00100_00100;
                "Y":glyph=35'b10001_10001_01010_00100_00100_00100_00100;
                "0":glyph=35'b01110_10001_10011_10101_11001_10001_01110;
                "1":glyph=35'b00100_01100_00100_00100_00100_00100_01110;
                "2":glyph=35'b01110_10001_00001_00010_00100_01000_11111;
                "3":glyph=35'b11110_00001_00001_01110_00001_00001_11110;
                "4":glyph=35'b00010_00110_01010_10010_11111_00010_00010;
                "5":glyph=35'b11111_10000_10000_11110_00001_00001_11110;
                "6":glyph=35'b01110_10000_10000_11110_10001_10001_01110;
                "7":glyph=35'b11111_00001_00010_00100_01000_01000_01000;
                "8":glyph=35'b01110_10001_10001_01110_10001_10001_01110;
                "9":glyph=35'b01110_10001_10001_01111_00001_00001_01110;
                ":":glyph=35'b00000_00100_00100_00000_00100_00100_00000;
                ".":glyph=35'b00000_00000_00000_00000_00000_00100_00100;
                "-":glyph=35'b00000_00000_00000_11111_00000_00000_00000;
                default:glyph=0;
            endcase
        end
    endfunction
    function [7:0] hex_char;
        input [3:0] nibble;
        begin hex_char=nibble<10 ? 8'd48+nibble : 8'd65+nibble-10; end
    endfunction
    function [7:0] title_char;
        input integer n;
        begin
            title_char=" ";
            case(n)
                0:title_char="C";1:title_char="O";2:title_char="N";3:title_char="T";
                4:title_char="R";5:title_char="A";6:title_char="S";7:title_char="T";
                9:title_char="P";10:title_char="R";11:title_char="O";
                12:title_char="B";13:title_char="E";15:title_char="0";16:title_char="1";
                default:;
            endcase
        end
    endfunction
    function [7:0] base_char;
        input integer n;
        begin
            base_char=" ";
            case(n)
                0:base_char="L";1:base_char="E";2:base_char="F";3:base_char="T";
                5:base_char="B";6:base_char="A";7:base_char="S";8:base_char="E";
                default:;
            endcase
        end
    endfunction
    function [7:0] gain_char;
        input integer n;
        input [2:0] setting;
        begin
            gain_char=" ";
            case(n)
                0:gain_char="R";1:gain_char="I";2:gain_char="G";3:gain_char="H";4:gain_char="T";
                6:gain_char=setting==4 ? "2" : "1";
                7:gain_char=".";
                8:case(setting)
                    1:gain_char="2";2:gain_char="5";3:gain_char="7";default:gain_char="0";
                  endcase
                9:gain_char=(setting==1 || setting==3) ? "5" : "0";
                11:gain_char="K";12:gain_char="E";13:gain_char="Y";14:gain_char="1";
                default:;
            endcase
        end
    endfunction

    integer local_x, local_y, index, col, row, field_pos;
    reg [7:0] character;
    reg [9:0] mean;
    reg [34:0] dots;
    reg text_area;
    always @* begin
        rgb=input_rgb;
        local_x=0;local_y=0;index=0;col=0;row=0;field_pos=0;
        character=" ";mean=0;dots=0;text_area=0;
        if (x>=640 && x<1280 && y>=120 && y<600)
            rgb={contrast8(input_rgb[23:16],level),contrast8(input_rgb[15:8],level),
                 contrast8(input_rgb[7:0],level)};

        if(y>=644 && y<658) begin
            if(x>=8 && x<8+17*12) begin
                local_x=x-8;local_y=y-644;text_area=1;
                character=title_char(local_x/12);
            end else if(x>=648 && x<648+9*12) begin
                local_x=x-648;local_y=y-644;text_area=1;
                character=base_char(local_x/12);
            end
        end else if(y>=664 && y<678) begin
            if(x>=8 && x<8+23*12) begin
                local_x=x-8;local_y=y-664;text_area=1;
                index=local_x/12;field_pos=index%6;
                case(index/6)
                    0:mean=cfa_means[9:0];
                    1:mean=cfa_means[19:10];
                    2:mean=cfa_means[29:20];
                    default:mean=cfa_means[39:30];
                endcase
                if(field_pos==0) character=8'd65+index/6;
                else if(field_pos==1) character=":";
                else if(field_pos>=2 && field_pos<=4)
                    character=sample_valid ? hex_char((mean >> ((4-field_pos)*4)) & 4'hF) : "-";
            end else if(x>=648 && x<648+15*12) begin
                local_x=x-648;local_y=y-664;text_area=1;
                character=gain_char(local_x/12,level);
            end
        end
        col=(local_x%12)/2;row=local_y/2;dots=glyph(character);
        if(text_area && col<5 && row<7 && dots[34-row*5-col]) rgb=24'hE0E0E0;

        // Standard references are direct display RGB, independent of the level.
        if(y>=692 && y<712 && x<1280) case(x[10:8])
            0:rgb=24'hFF0000;
            1:rgb=24'h00FF00;
            2:rgb=24'h0000FF;
            3:rgb=24'hFFFFFF;
            default:rgb=24'h808080;
        endcase
    end

`ifndef SYNTHESIS
    initial begin
        if(DEBOUNCE_CYCLES<1 || INITIAL_LEVEL<0 || INITIAL_LEVEL>4)
            $fatal(1,"Invalid contrast-probe debounce or initial level");
    end
`endif
endmodule
异步 FIFO style_async_fifo跨域

隔离摄像头、DDR 核心和显示时钟，保持数据及帧标志一同跨域。

时钟域：写时钟域 ↔ 读时钟域。复位极性以实际端口及下方源码为准。

实现逻辑

双口 RAM 使用本域二进制指针寻址，Gray 指针经两级同步传到另一域以判断空/满。读端先把 RAM 字注册到 read-ahead 寄存器，再用 m_valid 交付。容量是 2^ADDR_BITS 个 RAM 字加一个预取字。

握手与时序

写端 s_valid&s_ready 接受一字；读端 m_valid&m_ready 消费一字。读端预取输出在 ready=0 时保持。wr_level 是 RAM 所有权占用量，rd_level 包含已预取的字。

状态与流程

full、out_valid 和 wbin/rbin 指针构成状态，不依赖厂商 FIFO IP。

错误处理与恢复

必须共同复位两端，不能只清一个指针。异步断言复位，各域通过 style_reset_sync 同步释放；复位未释放时 ready/valid 为 0。

实际参数

参数源码默认值
WIDTH32
ADDR_BITS10

全部实际端口

端口方向位宽声明用途与约定
wr_clkinput1FIFO写入时钟
rd_clkinput1FIFO读出时钟
async_rst_ninput1FIFO两端共同低有效异步复位输入
s_datainput[WIDTH-1:0]输入流：打包数据
s_validinput1输入流：有效，握手才推进
s_readyoutput1输入流：接收就绪
m_dataoutput[WIDTH-1:0]输出流：打包数据
m_validoutput1输出流：有效，握手才推进
m_readyinput1输出流：接收就绪
wr_leveloutput[ADDR_BITS:0]FIFO内RAM字占用量，跨域有同步延迟
rd_leveloutput[ADDR_BITS:0]FIFO读域可用量，包含读端预取字

源文件：rtl/style_async_fifo.sv；下方内嵌当前源码，可直接在网页搜索。

展开完整源码
// Portable dual-clock FIFO. A registered read-ahead word provides ready/valid
// semantics; the read pointer releases RAM only after that word is copied.
// Capacity is 2**ADDR_BITS RAM words plus the read-ahead register. Reset both
// domains together: flushing one side alone is deliberately unsupported.
module style_async_fifo #(
    parameter integer WIDTH = 32, parameter integer ADDR_BITS = 10
)(
    input wire wr_clk, rd_clk, async_rst_n,
    input wire [WIDTH-1:0] s_data, input wire s_valid, output wire s_ready,
    output wire [WIDTH-1:0] m_data, output wire m_valid, input wire m_ready,
    output wire [ADDR_BITS:0] wr_level, rd_level
);
    localparam integer DEPTH = 1 << ADDR_BITS;
    reg [WIDTH-1:0] memory [0:DEPTH-1];
    wire wr_rst_n, rd_rst_n;
    style_reset_sync wr_reset(wr_clk, async_rst_n, wr_rst_n);
    style_reset_sync rd_reset(rd_clk, async_rst_n, rd_rst_n);
    reg [ADDR_BITS:0] wbin, wgray, rbin, rgray;
    (* async_reg = "true" *) reg [ADDR_BITS:0] rgray_w1, rgray_w2;
    (* async_reg = "true" *) reg [ADDR_BITS:0] wgray_r1, wgray_r2;
    reg full, out_valid;
    reg [WIDTH-1:0] out_data;
    function [ADDR_BITS:0] gray_to_bin;
        input [ADDR_BITS:0] value;
        integer k;
        begin
            gray_to_bin[ADDR_BITS] = value[ADDR_BITS];
            for (k=ADDR_BITS-1; k>=0; k=k-1)
                gray_to_bin[k] = gray_to_bin[k+1] ^ value[k];
        end
    endfunction
    wire push = s_valid && s_ready;
    wire [ADDR_BITS:0] wnext = wbin + push;
    wire [ADDR_BITS:0] wgnext = (wnext >> 1) ^ wnext;
    wire empty = rgray == wgray_r2;
    wire load = (!out_valid || m_ready) && !empty && rd_rst_n;
    wire [ADDR_BITS:0] rnext = rbin + 1'b1;
    assign s_ready = wr_rst_n && !full;
    assign m_valid = rd_rst_n && out_valid;
    assign m_data = out_data;
    assign wr_level = wbin - gray_to_bin(rgray_w2);
    assign rd_level = gray_to_bin(wgray_r2) - rbin + out_valid;
    always @(posedge wr_clk or negedge wr_rst_n) begin
        if (!wr_rst_n) begin
            wbin<=0; wgray<=0; full<=0; rgray_w1<=0; rgray_w2<=0;
        end else begin
            rgray_w1<=rgray; rgray_w2<=rgray_w1;
            wbin<=wnext; wgray<=wgnext;
            full <= wgnext == {~rgray_w2[ADDR_BITS:ADDR_BITS-1],rgray_w2[ADDR_BITS-2:0]};
        end
    end
    always @(posedge wr_clk) if (push) memory[wbin[ADDR_BITS-1:0]] <= s_data;
    always @(posedge rd_clk or negedge rd_rst_n) begin
        if (!rd_rst_n) begin
            rbin<=0; rgray<=0; wgray_r1<=0; wgray_r2<=0; out_valid<=0;
        end else begin
            wgray_r1<=wgray; wgray_r2<=wgray_r1;
            if (load) begin
                rbin<=rnext; rgray<=(rnext>>1)^rnext; out_valid<=1;
            end else if (out_valid && m_ready) out_valid<=0;
        end
    end
    // Separate synchronous RAM read process enables Efinity BRAM inference.
    always @(posedge rd_clk) if (load) out_data <= memory[rbin[ADDR_BITS-1:0]];
endmodule
复位同步释放 style_reset_sync跨域

允许共同复位立即断言，避免异步撤销复位破坏本域状态。

时钟域：每个使用它的本地时钟域。复位极性以实际端口及下方源码为准。

实现逻辑

异步低有效输入清零同步移位链，复位撤回后经过本域若干上升沿才输出有效 rst_n。

握手与时序

复位没有 ready/valid。每个异步 FIFO 的写域、读域各有独立实例。

状态与流程

同步移位链。

错误处理与恢复

PLL 失锁、DDR 未就绪或顶层复位时重新断言；恢复启动条件由平台顶层控制。

实际参数

参数源码默认值
STAGES3

全部实际端口

端口方向位宽声明用途与约定
clkinput1本模块核心/摄像头工作时钟，所属域见上文
async_rst_ninput1FIFO两端共同低有效异步复位输入
rst_noutput1本域低有效复位

源文件：rtl/style_reset_sync.sv；下方内嵌当前源码，可直接在网页搜索。

展开完整源码
// Reset is asynchronous on assertion and local-clock synchronous on release.
module style_reset_sync #(parameter integer STAGES = 3)(
    input wire clk, input wire async_rst_n, output wire rst_n
);
    (* async_reg = "true" *) reg [STAGES-1:0] pipe;
    always @(posedge clk or negedge async_rst_n)
        if (!async_rst_n) pipe <= 0;
        else pipe <= {pipe[STAGES-2:0], 1'b1};
    assign rst_n = pipe[STAGES-1];
endmodule
计数诊断跨域 style_cdc_counter跨域

把摄像头和显示统计安全送到核心寄存器，避免直接采样变化的 32 位二进制数。

时钟域：源计数域 → 核心寄存器域。复位极性以实际端口及下方源码为准。

实现逻辑

源域注册 Gray 编码，经两级目的域同步，再组合解码为二进制。显示诊断会有数拍延迟。

握手与时序

只适用于每个源时钟最多增加 1 的单调计数；不适合任意变化的配置字、帧地址或一拍跳多个计数。

状态与流程

源 Gray 寄存器、目的同步级；无任务状态机。

错误处理与恢复

两域计数与同步链应按同一个系统复位重新起点；诊断数不是事务完成握手。

实际参数

参数源码默认值
WIDTH32

全部实际端口

端口方向位宽声明用途与约定
src_clkinput1诊断计数的源时钟
src_rst_ninput1源域低有效复位
src_countinput[WIDTH-1:0]每源周期最多增1的原始计数
dst_clkinput1诊断目的时钟
dst_rst_ninput1目的域低有效复位
dst_countoutput[WIDTH-1:0]Gray同步后恢复的目的域计数

源文件：rtl/style_cdc_counter.sv；下方内嵌当前源码，可直接在网页搜索。

展开完整源码
// Only for monotonically incrementing counters (one count per source edge).
module style_cdc_counter #(parameter integer WIDTH=32)(
    input wire src_clk, src_rst_n, input wire [WIDTH-1:0] src_count,
    input wire dst_clk, dst_rst_n, output reg [WIDTH-1:0] dst_count
);
    reg [WIDTH-1:0] gray;
    (* async_reg="true" *) reg [WIDTH-1:0] sync1,sync2;
    integer k;
    always @(posedge src_clk or negedge src_rst_n)
        if(!src_rst_n) gray<=0; else gray<=(src_count>>1)^src_count;
    always @(posedge dst_clk or negedge dst_rst_n)
        if(!dst_rst_n) begin sync1<=0; sync2<=0; end
        else begin sync1<=gray; sync2<=sync1; end
    always @* begin
        dst_count[WIDTH-1]=sync2[WIDTH-1];
        for(k=WIDTH-2;k>=0;k=k-1) dst_count[k]=dst_count[k+1]^sync2[k];
    end
endmodule
RGB888 → AXI 打包 style_pixel_packer存储

把四个 RGB888 像素整理成一拍 128 位 DDR 写数据。

时钟域：核心 DDR 时钟域。复位极性以实际端口及下方源码为准。

实现逻辑

每像素形成字 0x00RRGGBB；第一个像素在 bits31:0，第四个在 bits127:96。lane 按 0..3 累积，四像素后给出 m_data。当前整帧尺寸是 4 的倍数，因此没有有效帧尾 padding。

握手与时序

输出等待时保持 m_valid/m_data/m_last；s_ready 管输入。s_last 是整帧最后像素，不是每行结束。clear 用于新任务或丢弃半帧。

状态与流程

lane、pack_data、输出 valid。

错误处理与恢复

clear 丢弃未完成打包和待输出字，只能用于上层已决定取消的帧，不能在有效 AXI 事务中直接撤回数据。

实际参数

无可配置模块参数。

全部实际端口

端口方向位宽声明用途与约定
clkinput1本模块核心/摄像头工作时钟，所属域见上文
rst_ninput1本域低有效复位
clearinput1取消本地缓存内容，高有效；上层须先保证事务安全
s_rgbinput[23:0]输入流：RGB888像素
s_validinput1输入流：有效，握手才推进
s_lastinput1输入流：命令/帧最后数据
s_readyoutput1输入流：接收就绪
m_dataoutput[127:0]输出流：打包数据
m_validoutput1输出流：有效，握手才推进
m_lastoutput1输出流：命令/帧最后数据
m_readyinput1输出流：接收就绪

源文件：rtl/style_pixel_packer.sv；下方内嵌当前源码，可直接在网页搜索。

展开完整源码
// DDR layout: one pixel occupies a little-endian word 0x00RRGGBB.
// Four consecutive pixels become one 128-bit AXI beat, earliest in bits31:0.
module style_pixel_packer(
    input wire clk,rst_n,clear,
    input wire [23:0] s_rgb, input wire s_valid,s_last, output wire s_ready,
    output wire [127:0] m_data, output wire m_valid,m_last,input wire m_ready
);
    reg [127:0] pack_data,out_data;
    reg [1:0] lane;
    reg valid,last;
    assign s_ready = !valid || m_ready;
    assign m_data=out_data; assign m_valid=valid; assign m_last=last;
    always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
        if(!rst_n) begin lane<=0; valid<=0; last<=0; pack_data<=0; out_data<=0; end
        else if(clear) begin lane<=0; valid<=0; last<=0; pack_data<=0; end
        else begin
            if(valid && m_ready) valid<=0;
            if(s_valid && s_ready) begin
                pack_data[lane*32+:32]<={8'h00,s_rgb};
                if(lane==3 || s_last) begin
                    case(lane)
                        0:out_data<={96'b0,8'b0,s_rgb};
                        1:out_data<={64'b0,8'b0,s_rgb,pack_data[31:0]};
                        2:out_data<={32'b0,8'b0,s_rgb,pack_data[63:0]};
                        3:out_data<={8'b0,s_rgb,pack_data[95:0]};
                    endcase
                    valid<=1;last<=s_last;lane<=0;pack_data<=0;
                end else lane<=lane+1'b1;
            end
        end
    end
endmodule
AXI → RGB888 解包 style_pixel_unpacker存储

把 128 位读数据还原成四个顺序像素，供计算或显示使用。

时钟域：核心 DDR 时钟域。复位极性以实际端口及下方源码为准。

实现逻辑

按 lane=0..3 取各 32 位字的低 24 位，忽略高 8 位 RGBX padding；末拍 m_last 只在第四个像素上有效。

握手与时序

m_valid&m_ready 才推进 lane；上一个字最后像素被接收时可以同时接下一个 128 位字。

状态与流程

有效字寄存器及 lane 计数。

错误处理与恢复

clear 取消本地待输出字；读 DMA 的 AXI 事务仍须先按协议 drain。

实际参数

无可配置模块参数。

全部实际端口

端口方向位宽声明用途与约定
clkinput1本模块核心/摄像头工作时钟，所属域见上文
rst_ninput1本域低有效复位
clearinput1取消本地缓存内容，高有效；上层须先保证事务安全
s_datainput[127:0]输入流：打包数据
s_validinput1输入流：有效，握手才推进
s_lastinput1输入流：命令/帧最后数据
s_readyoutput1输入流：接收就绪
m_rgboutput[23:0]输出流：RGB888像素
m_validoutput1输出流：有效，握手才推进
m_lastoutput1输出流：命令/帧最后数据
m_readyinput1输出流：接收就绪

源文件：rtl/style_pixel_unpacker.sv；下方内嵌当前源码，可直接在网页搜索。

展开完整源码
module style_pixel_unpacker(
    input wire clk,rst_n,clear,
    input wire [127:0] s_data,input wire s_valid,s_last,output wire s_ready,
    output wire [23:0] m_rgb,output wire m_valid,m_last,input wire m_ready
);
    reg [127:0] data;
    reg valid,last;
    reg [1:0] lane;
    assign m_valid=valid; assign m_rgb=data[lane*32+:24];
    assign m_last=last && lane==3;
    assign s_ready=!valid || (m_ready && lane==3);
    always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
        if(!rst_n) begin valid<=0;last<=0;lane<=0;data<=0;end
        else if(clear) begin valid<=0;last<=0;lane<=0;end
        else begin
            if(valid && m_ready) begin
                if(lane==3) begin valid<=0;lane<=0;end else lane<=lane+1'b1;
            end
            if(s_valid && s_ready) begin data<=s_data;last<=s_last;valid<=1;lane<=0;end
        end
    end
endmodule
突发写 DMA style_axi_write_dma存储

把连续 128 位数据写到一段 DDR，采集和结果各用一个实例。

时钟域：核心 DDR 时钟域。复位极性以实际端口及下方源码为准。

实现逻辑

命令给地址和总拍数；先收满一次 burst 的数据，再独立完成 AW 和 W 握手，最后检查 B。每 burst 不超过 BURST_BEATS，不跨 4 KiB，所有 byte strobe 有效。只有最后一拍 B 返回 OKAY，整帧才 done 且 error=0。

握手与时序

cmd_valid/cmd_ready 接命令；s_valid/s_ready 收数据；AWVALID/WVALID 一旦提供就不能因 abort 或超时撤回；BREADY 接收写响应。

状态与流程

IDLE → FILL → ISSUE → RESPONSE；每 burst 回 FILL，整任务回 IDLE。

错误处理与恢复

错误码 1 命令、2 abort、3 LAST、4 RESP、5 ID、6 timeout。已发出的写事务必须完成 B；没有响应的 timeout 置 sticky_fault，禁止新命令，要求复位而不是直接重用 AXI。失败帧绝不发布。

实际参数

参数源码默认值
ADDR_WIDTH32
DATA_WIDTH128
ID_WIDTH6
BURST_BEATS16
AXI_ID0
TIMEOUT_CYCLES1000000

全部实际端口

端口方向位宽声明用途与约定
clkinput1本模块核心/摄像头工作时钟，所属域见上文
rst_ninput1本域低有效复位
cmd_validinput1命令有效，与cmd_ready握手后开始
cmd_readyoutput1可接受新命令；sticky fault后为0
cmd_addrinput[ADDR_WIDTH-1:0]命令起始DDR字节地址，按AXI拍对齐
cmd_beatsinput[31:0]本次命令的AXI数据总拍数，非字节数
abortinput1请求终止当前DMA，已提供AXI事务仍按协议排空
busyoutput1任务进行中
doneoutput1本域单拍任务结束事件，须与error同看
erroroutput1任务结束失败事件
error_codeoutput[7:0]任务失败原因码，见恢复说明
sticky_faultoutput1不可安全复用总线的故障锁存，需复位
s_datainput[DATA_WIDTH-1:0]输入流：打包数据
s_validinput1输入流：有效，握手才推进
s_readyoutput1输入流：接收就绪
s_lastinput1输入流：命令/帧最后数据
m_axi_awidoutput[ID_WIDTH-1:0]AXI 写地址 事务ID
m_axi_awaddroutput[ADDR_WIDTH-1:0]AXI 写地址 字节地址
m_axi_awlenoutput[7:0]AXI 写地址 burst拍数减1
m_axi_awsizeoutput[2:0]AXI 写地址 每拍字节数log2
m_axi_awburstoutput[1:0]AXI 写地址 地址模式，INCR=01
m_axi_awvalidoutput1AXI 写地址 有效，等待ready时保持
m_axi_awreadyinput1AXI 写地址 接收方就绪
m_axi_wdataoutput[DATA_WIDTH-1:0]AXI 写数据 数据
m_axi_wstrboutput[DATA_WIDTH/8-1:0]AXI 写数据 字节写使能
m_axi_wlastoutput1AXI 写数据 本burst最后数据拍
m_axi_wvalidoutput1AXI 写数据 有效，等待ready时保持
m_axi_wreadyinput1AXI 写数据 接收方就绪
m_axi_bidinput[ID_WIDTH-1:0]AXI 写响应 事务ID
m_axi_brespinput[1:0]AXI 写响应 响应码，OKAY=00
m_axi_bvalidinput1AXI 写响应 有效，等待ready时保持
m_axi_breadyoutput1AXI 写响应 接收方就绪

源文件：rtl/style_axi_write_dma.sv；下方内嵌当前源码，可直接在网页搜索。

展开完整源码
// Portable AXI4 frame writer. One outstanding burst; no vendor primitives.
// A command is DATA_WIDTH/8-byte aligned and contains whole, fully strobed beats.
// Invalid/aborted frames must not be published by the caller.
module style_axi_write_dma #(
    parameter integer ADDR_WIDTH = 32,
    parameter integer DATA_WIDTH = 128,
    parameter integer ID_WIDTH = 6,
    parameter integer BURST_BEATS = 16,
    parameter integer AXI_ID = 0,
    parameter integer TIMEOUT_CYCLES = 1000000
) (
    input wire clk, input wire rst_n,
    input wire cmd_valid, output wire cmd_ready,
    input wire [ADDR_WIDTH-1:0] cmd_addr,
    input wire [31:0] cmd_beats,
    input wire abort,
    output wire busy, output reg done, output reg error,
    output reg [7:0] error_code, output reg sticky_fault,
    input wire [DATA_WIDTH-1:0] s_data,
    input wire s_valid, output wire s_ready, input wire s_last,
    output wire [ID_WIDTH-1:0] m_axi_awid,
    output wire [ADDR_WIDTH-1:0] m_axi_awaddr,
    output wire [7:0] m_axi_awlen,
    output wire [2:0] m_axi_awsize,
    output wire [1:0] m_axi_awburst,
    output wire m_axi_awvalid, input wire m_axi_awready,
    output wire [DATA_WIDTH-1:0] m_axi_wdata,
    output wire [DATA_WIDTH/8-1:0] m_axi_wstrb,
    output wire m_axi_wlast, output wire m_axi_wvalid,
    input wire m_axi_wready,
    input wire [ID_WIDTH-1:0] m_axi_bid,
    input wire [1:0] m_axi_bresp,
    input wire m_axi_bvalid, output wire m_axi_bready
);
    localparam integer BYTES = DATA_WIDTH / 8;
    localparam integer SIZE = $clog2(BYTES);
    localparam [7:0] E_COMMAND = 8'h01, E_ABORT = 8'h02,
                     E_LAST = 8'h03, E_RESPONSE = 8'h04,
                     E_ID = 8'h05, E_TIMEOUT = 8'h06;
    localparam [2:0] IDLE = 0, FILL = 1, ISSUE = 2, RESPONSE = 3;
    reg [2:0] state;
    reg [DATA_WIDTH-1:0] buffer [0:BURST_BEATS-1];
    reg [ADDR_WIDTH-1:0] addr;
    reg [31:0] remaining, burst_count, fill_count, send_count;
    reg aw_accepted, w_finished, task_failed;
    reg [7:0] task_code;
    reg [31:0] watchdog;
    wire aw_fire = m_axi_awvalid && m_axi_awready;
    wire w_fire = m_axi_wvalid && m_axi_wready;
    wire b_fire = m_axi_bvalid && m_axi_bready;
    wire stream_fire = s_valid && s_ready;
    wire progress = aw_fire || w_fire || b_fire || stream_fire;
    wire timeout_now = (TIMEOUT_CYCLES > 0) && state != IDLE &&
                       !progress && watchdog >= TIMEOUT_CYCLES-1;
    wire stop_now = task_failed || abort || timeout_now;

    // Compute this at burst boundaries only. AXI bursts never cross 4 KiB.
    function [31:0] choose_burst;
        input [ADDR_WIDTH-1:0] address;
        input [31:0] beats;
        reg [31:0] boundary_beats;
        reg [31:0] n;
        begin
            boundary_beats = (4096 - {20'b0, address[11:0]}) / BYTES;
            n = beats < BURST_BEATS ? beats : BURST_BEATS;
            choose_burst = n < boundary_beats ? n : boundary_beats;
        end
    endfunction

    assign cmd_ready = state == IDLE && !sticky_fault;
    assign busy = state != IDLE;
    assign s_ready = state == FILL && !task_failed && !abort;
    assign m_axi_awid = AXI_ID;
    assign m_axi_awaddr = addr;
    assign m_axi_awlen = burst_count[7:0] - 1'b1;
    assign m_axi_awsize = SIZE;
    assign m_axi_awburst = 2'b01;
    assign m_axi_awvalid = state == ISSUE && !aw_accepted;
    assign m_axi_wdata = buffer[send_count];
    assign m_axi_wstrb = {BYTES{1'b1}};
    assign m_axi_wlast = send_count == burst_count-1;
    assign m_axi_wvalid = state == ISSUE && !w_finished;
    assign m_axi_bready = state == RESPONSE;

    always @(posedge clk) begin
        if (!rst_n) begin
            state <= IDLE;
            addr <= 0; remaining <= 0; burst_count <= 1;
            fill_count <= 0; send_count <= 0;
            aw_accepted <= 0; w_finished <= 0;
            task_failed <= 0; task_code <= 0; watchdog <= 0;
            done <= 0; error <= 0; error_code <= 0; sticky_fault <= 0;
        end else begin
            done <= 0;
            error <= 0;
            if (state == IDLE || progress) watchdog <= 0;
            else if (!timeout_now) watchdog <= watchdog + 1'b1;
            if (abort && state != IDLE && !task_failed) begin
                task_failed <= 1;
                task_code <= E_ABORT;
            end
            if (timeout_now) begin
                sticky_fault <= 1;
                task_failed <= 1;
                task_code <= E_TIMEOUT;
            end
            case (state)
                IDLE: if (cmd_valid && cmd_ready) begin
                    task_failed <= 0; task_code <= 0; error_code <= 0;
                    if (cmd_beats == 0 || (cmd_addr & (BYTES-1)) != 0) begin
                        done <= 1; error <= 1; error_code <= E_COMMAND;
                    end else if (abort) begin
                        done <= 1; error <= 1; error_code <= E_ABORT;
                    end else begin
                        addr <= cmd_addr; remaining <= cmd_beats;
                        burst_count <= choose_burst(cmd_addr, cmd_beats);
                        fill_count <= 0; state <= FILL;
                    end
                end
                FILL: begin
                    if (stop_now) begin
                        state <= IDLE; done <= 1; error <= 1;
                        error_code <= timeout_now ? E_TIMEOUT :
                                      task_failed ? task_code : E_ABORT;
                    end else if (stream_fire) begin
                        if (s_last != (remaining == fill_count+1)) begin
                            state <= IDLE; done <= 1; error <= 1;
                            error_code <= E_LAST;
                        end else begin
                            buffer[fill_count] <= s_data;
                            if (fill_count == burst_count-1) begin
                                send_count <= 0;
                                aw_accepted <= 0; w_finished <= 0;
                                state <= ISSUE;
                            end else fill_count <= fill_count + 1'b1;
                        end
                    end
                end
                ISSUE: begin
                    // Abort/timeouts do not revoke AWVALID or any part of W.
                    if (aw_fire) aw_accepted <= 1;
                    if (w_fire) begin
                        if (send_count == burst_count-1) w_finished <= 1;
                        else send_count <= send_count + 1'b1;
                    end
                    if ((aw_accepted || aw_fire) &&
                        (w_finished || (w_fire && m_axi_wlast))) state <= RESPONSE;
                end
                RESPONSE: if (b_fire) begin
                    if (stop_now || m_axi_bresp != 0 || m_axi_bid != AXI_ID) begin
                        state <= IDLE; done <= 1; error <= 1;
                        error_code <= timeout_now ? E_TIMEOUT :
                                      task_failed ? task_code : abort ? E_ABORT :
                                      m_axi_bid != AXI_ID ? E_ID : E_RESPONSE;
                    end else if (remaining == burst_count) begin
                        state <= IDLE; done <= 1; error <= 0; error_code <= 0;
                    end else begin
                        addr <= addr + burst_count*BYTES;
                        remaining <= remaining - burst_count;
                        burst_count <= choose_burst(addr + burst_count*BYTES,
                                                    remaining - burst_count);
                        fill_count <= 0; state <= FILL;
                    end
                end
                default: state <= IDLE;
            endcase
        end
    end
endmodule
突发读 DMA style_axi_read_dma存储

为计算和显示读出 DDR 数据，每个实例只保留一个未完成 burst。

时钟域：核心 DDR 时钟域。复位极性以实际端口及下方源码为准。

实现逻辑

发AR后先在burst buffer收完整段，检查每拍RID/RRESP/RLAST，再提供m_valid，坏响应不先进入图像FIFO。通用模块默认BURST_BEATS=16；平台读实例用READ_BURST_BEATS，现默认128，写实例仍16。较大读burst分摊每次首读等待，地址对齐与4KiB边界仍受检查。

握手与时序

cmd_valid/cmd_ready 接命令；ARVALID 保持到 ARREADY；RREADY drain 已接事务；m_valid/m_ready 输出已验证数据。m_last 是该命令的最后一拍。

状态与流程

IDLE → ADDRESS → RECEIVE → OUTPUT；每段验证后 OUTPUT，剩余段再 ADDRESS。

错误处理与恢复

错误码同写 DMA。错误或 abort 仍 drain 到真实 RLAST；timeout 为 sticky_fault，不能凭计时器假设总线事务消失。已经提供且被背压的输出拍仍保持稳定。

实际参数

参数源码默认值
ADDR_WIDTH32
DATA_WIDTH128
ID_WIDTH6
BURST_BEATS16
AXI_ID0
TIMEOUT_CYCLES1000000

全部实际端口

端口方向位宽声明用途与约定
clkinput1本模块核心/摄像头工作时钟，所属域见上文
rst_ninput1本域低有效复位
cmd_validinput1命令有效，与cmd_ready握手后开始
cmd_readyoutput1可接受新命令；sticky fault后为0
cmd_addrinput[ADDR_WIDTH-1:0]命令起始DDR字节地址，按AXI拍对齐
cmd_beatsinput[31:0]本次命令的AXI数据总拍数，非字节数
abortinput1请求终止当前DMA，已提供AXI事务仍按协议排空
busyoutput1任务进行中
doneoutput1本域单拍任务结束事件，须与error同看
erroroutput1任务结束失败事件
error_codeoutput[7:0]任务失败原因码，见恢复说明
sticky_faultoutput1不可安全复用总线的故障锁存，需复位
m_dataoutput[DATA_WIDTH-1:0]输出流：打包数据
m_validoutput1输出流：有效，握手才推进
m_readyinput1输出流：接收就绪
m_lastoutput1输出流：命令/帧最后数据
m_axi_aridoutput[ID_WIDTH-1:0]AXI 读地址 事务ID
m_axi_araddroutput[ADDR_WIDTH-1:0]AXI 读地址 字节地址
m_axi_arlenoutput[7:0]AXI 读地址 burst拍数减1
m_axi_arsizeoutput[2:0]AXI 读地址 每拍字节数log2
m_axi_arburstoutput[1:0]AXI 读地址 地址模式，INCR=01
m_axi_arvalidoutput1AXI 读地址 有效，等待ready时保持
m_axi_arreadyinput1AXI 读地址 接收方就绪
m_axi_ridinput[ID_WIDTH-1:0]AXI 读数据 事务ID
m_axi_rdatainput[DATA_WIDTH-1:0]AXI 读数据 数据
m_axi_rrespinput[1:0]AXI 读数据 响应码，OKAY=00
m_axi_rlastinput1AXI 读数据 本burst最后数据拍
m_axi_rvalidinput1AXI 读数据 有效，等待ready时保持
m_axi_rreadyoutput1AXI 读数据 接收方就绪

源文件：rtl/style_axi_read_dma.sv；下方内嵌当前源码，可直接在网页搜索。

展开完整源码
// Portable AXI4 frame reader, one outstanding burst. Each <=BURST_BEATS
// burst is checked completely before any of its data is made available.
module style_axi_read_dma #(
    parameter integer ADDR_WIDTH = 32,
    parameter integer DATA_WIDTH = 128,
    parameter integer ID_WIDTH = 6,
    parameter integer BURST_BEATS = 16,
    parameter integer AXI_ID = 0,
    parameter integer TIMEOUT_CYCLES = 1000000
) (
    input wire clk, input wire rst_n,
    input wire cmd_valid, output wire cmd_ready,
    input wire [ADDR_WIDTH-1:0] cmd_addr,
    input wire [31:0] cmd_beats,
    input wire abort,
    output wire busy, output reg done, output reg error,
    output reg [7:0] error_code, output reg sticky_fault,
    output wire [DATA_WIDTH-1:0] m_data,
    output wire m_valid, input wire m_ready, output wire m_last,
    output wire [ID_WIDTH-1:0] m_axi_arid,
    output wire [ADDR_WIDTH-1:0] m_axi_araddr,
    output wire [7:0] m_axi_arlen,
    output wire [2:0] m_axi_arsize,
    output wire [1:0] m_axi_arburst,
    output wire m_axi_arvalid, input wire m_axi_arready,
    input wire [ID_WIDTH-1:0] m_axi_rid,
    input wire [DATA_WIDTH-1:0] m_axi_rdata,
    input wire [1:0] m_axi_rresp,
    input wire m_axi_rlast, input wire m_axi_rvalid,
    output wire m_axi_rready
);
    localparam integer BYTES = DATA_WIDTH / 8;
    localparam integer SIZE = $clog2(BYTES);
    localparam [7:0] E_COMMAND = 8'h01, E_ABORT = 8'h02,
                     E_LAST = 8'h03, E_RESPONSE = 8'h04,
                     E_ID = 8'h05, E_TIMEOUT = 8'h06;
    localparam [2:0] IDLE = 0, ADDRESS = 1, RECEIVE = 2, OUTPUT = 3;
    reg [2:0] state;
    reg [DATA_WIDTH-1:0] buffer [0:BURST_BEATS-1];
    reg [ADDR_WIDTH-1:0] addr;
    reg [31:0] remaining, burst_count, receive_count, output_count;
    reg task_failed;
    reg [7:0] task_code;
    reg [31:0] watchdog;
    wire ar_fire = m_axi_arvalid && m_axi_arready;
    wire r_fire = m_axi_rvalid && m_axi_rready;
    wire output_fire = m_valid && m_ready;
    wire progress = ar_fire || r_fire || output_fire;
    wire timeout_now = (TIMEOUT_CYCLES > 0) && state != IDLE &&
                       !progress && watchdog >= TIMEOUT_CYCLES-1;
    wire stop_now = task_failed || abort || timeout_now;
    wire beat_error = m_axi_rresp != 0 || m_axi_rid != AXI_ID ||
                      m_axi_rlast != (receive_count == burst_count-1) ||
                      receive_count >= burst_count;
    function [31:0] choose_burst;
        input [ADDR_WIDTH-1:0] address;
        input [31:0] beats;
        reg [31:0] boundary_beats;
        reg [31:0] n;
        begin
            boundary_beats = (4096 - {20'b0, address[11:0]}) / BYTES;
            n = beats < BURST_BEATS ? beats : BURST_BEATS;
            choose_burst = n < boundary_beats ? n : boundary_beats;
        end
    endfunction
    assign cmd_ready = state == IDLE && !sticky_fault;
    assign busy = state != IDLE;
    assign m_axi_arid = AXI_ID;
    assign m_axi_araddr = addr;
    assign m_axi_arlen = burst_count[7:0] - 1'b1;
    assign m_axi_arsize = SIZE;
    assign m_axi_arburst = 2'b01;
    assign m_axi_arvalid = state == ADDRESS;
    assign m_axi_rready = state == RECEIVE;
    assign m_data = buffer[output_count];
    assign m_valid = state == OUTPUT;
    assign m_last = remaining == burst_count && output_count == burst_count-1;

    always @(posedge clk) begin
        if (!rst_n) begin
            state <= IDLE; addr <= 0; remaining <= 0; burst_count <= 1;
            receive_count <= 0; output_count <= 0;
            task_failed <= 0; task_code <= 0; watchdog <= 0;
            done <= 0; error <= 0; error_code <= 0; sticky_fault <= 0;
        end else begin
            done <= 0; error <= 0;
            if (state == IDLE || progress) watchdog <= 0;
            else if (!timeout_now) watchdog <= watchdog + 1'b1;
            if (abort && state != IDLE && !task_failed) begin
                task_failed <= 1; task_code <= E_ABORT;
            end
            if (timeout_now) begin
                sticky_fault <= 1; task_failed <= 1; task_code <= E_TIMEOUT;
            end
            case (state)
                IDLE: if (cmd_valid && cmd_ready) begin
                    task_failed <= 0; task_code <= 0; error_code <= 0;
                    if (cmd_beats == 0 || (cmd_addr & (BYTES-1)) != 0) begin
                        done <= 1; error <= 1; error_code <= E_COMMAND;
                    end else if (abort) begin
                        done <= 1; error <= 1; error_code <= E_ABORT;
                    end else begin
                        addr <= cmd_addr; remaining <= cmd_beats;
                        burst_count <= choose_burst(cmd_addr, cmd_beats);
                        receive_count <= 0; state <= ADDRESS;
                    end
                end
                ADDRESS: if (ar_fire) begin
                    // Once ARVALID has been asserted it is held until acceptance.
                    receive_count <= 0; state <= RECEIVE;
                end
                RECEIVE: if (r_fire) begin
                    if (beat_error && !task_failed) begin
                        task_failed <= 1;
                        task_code <= m_axi_rid != AXI_ID ? E_ID :
                                     m_axi_rresp != 0 ? E_RESPONSE : E_LAST;
                    end
                    if (!stop_now && !beat_error)
                        buffer[receive_count] <= m_axi_rdata;
                    // Even on faults, drain through the actual RLAST. Timeout
                    // latches sticky_fault; it is not permission to reuse AXI.
                    if (m_axi_rlast) begin
                        if (stop_now || beat_error) begin
                            state <= IDLE; done <= 1; error <= 1;
                            error_code <= timeout_now ? E_TIMEOUT :
                                          task_failed ? task_code : abort ? E_ABORT :
                                          m_axi_rid != AXI_ID ? E_ID :
                                          m_axi_rresp != 0 ? E_RESPONSE : E_LAST;
                        end else begin
                            output_count <= 0; state <= OUTPUT;
                        end
                    end else if (receive_count != 32'hffffffff)
                        receive_count <= receive_count + 1'b1;
                end
                OUTPUT: if (output_fire) begin
                    // Do not revoke an already offered, stalled output beat.
                    if (stop_now) begin
                        state <= IDLE; done <= 1; error <= 1;
                        error_code <= timeout_now ? E_TIMEOUT :
                                      task_failed ? task_code : E_ABORT;
                    end else if (output_count == burst_count-1) begin
                        if (remaining == burst_count) begin
                            state <= IDLE; done <= 1; error <= 0; error_code <= 0;
                        end else begin
                            addr <= addr + burst_count*BYTES;
                            remaining <= remaining - burst_count;
                            burst_count <= choose_burst(addr + burst_count*BYTES,
                                                        remaining - burst_count);
                            receive_count <= 0; state <= ADDRESS;
                        end
                    end else output_count <= output_count + 1'b1;
                end
                default: state <= IDLE;
            endcase
        end
    end
endmodule
共享 DDR 仲裁 style_axi_arbiter存储

把两个写客户端和两个读客户端合到板载 DDR 的一个 AXI 接口。

时钟域：核心 DDR 时钟域。复位极性以实际端口及下方源码为准。

实现逻辑

写方向轮转公平分配，读方向偏向 client1 显示，但以 READ_CLIENT1_LIMIT 限制连续显示授权，避免计算永久饥饿。读写方向可各有一个 outstanding，独立运行。最小 slice 是 client0。

握手与时序

先注册事务 owner 再提供 AW/AR；等待期间不能换 owner。B 只送写 owner，R 只送读 owner；B 或 RLAST 握手后释放。

状态与流程

wr_active/wr_owner/aw_seen/w_seen 与 rd_active/rd_owner/ar_seen 两组状态。

错误处理与恢复

错误响应仍交给 owner DMA 判断；仲裁器不会替代 DDR 响应或自行取消无响应事务。

实际参数

参数源码默认值
ADDR_WIDTH32
DATA_WIDTH128
ID_WIDTH6
READ_CLIENT1_LIMIT4

全部实际端口

端口方向位宽声明用途与约定
clkinput1本模块核心/摄像头工作时钟，所属域见上文
rst_ninput1本域低有效复位
s_axi_awidinput[2*ID_WIDTH-1:0]AXI 写地址 事务ID；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_awaddrinput[2*ADDR_WIDTH-1:0]AXI 写地址 字节地址；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_awleninput[15:0]AXI 写地址 burst拍数减1；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_awsizeinput[5:0]AXI 写地址 每拍字节数log2；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_awburstinput[3:0]AXI 写地址 地址模式，INCR=01；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_awvalidinput[1:0]AXI 写地址 有效，等待ready时保持；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_awreadyoutput[1:0]AXI 写地址 接收方就绪；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_wdatainput[2*DATA_WIDTH-1:0]AXI 写数据 数据；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_wstrbinput[2*(DATA_WIDTH/8)-1:0]AXI 写数据 字节写使能；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_wlastinput[1:0]AXI 写数据 本burst最后数据拍；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_wvalidinput[1:0]AXI 写数据 有效，等待ready时保持；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_wreadyoutput[1:0]AXI 写数据 接收方就绪；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_bidoutput[2*ID_WIDTH-1:0]AXI 写响应 事务ID；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_brespoutput[3:0]AXI 写响应 响应码，OKAY=00；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_bvalidoutput[1:0]AXI 写响应 有效，等待ready时保持；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_breadyinput[1:0]AXI 写响应 接收方就绪；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_aridinput[2*ID_WIDTH-1:0]AXI 读地址 事务ID；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_araddrinput[2*ADDR_WIDTH-1:0]AXI 读地址 字节地址；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_arleninput[15:0]AXI 读地址 burst拍数减1；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_arsizeinput[5:0]AXI 读地址 每拍字节数log2；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_arburstinput[3:0]AXI 读地址 地址模式，INCR=01；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_arvalidinput[1:0]AXI 读地址 有效，等待ready时保持；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_arreadyoutput[1:0]AXI 读地址 接收方就绪；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_ridoutput[2*ID_WIDTH-1:0]AXI 读数据 事务ID；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_rdataoutput[2*DATA_WIDTH-1:0]AXI 读数据 数据；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_rrespoutput[3:0]AXI 读数据 响应码，OKAY=00；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_rlastoutput[1:0]AXI 读数据 本burst最后数据拍；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_rvalidoutput[1:0]AXI 读数据 有效，等待ready时保持；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
s_axi_rreadyinput[1:0]AXI 读数据 接收方就绪；两个客户端打平，client0在低slice
m_axi_awidoutput[ID_WIDTH-1:0]AXI 写地址 事务ID
m_axi_awaddroutput[ADDR_WIDTH-1:0]AXI 写地址 字节地址
m_axi_awlenoutput[7:0]AXI 写地址 burst拍数减1
m_axi_awsizeoutput[2:0]AXI 写地址 每拍字节数log2
m_axi_awburstoutput[1:0]AXI 写地址 地址模式，INCR=01
m_axi_awvalidoutput1AXI 写地址 有效，等待ready时保持
m_axi_awreadyinput1AXI 写地址 接收方就绪
m_axi_wdataoutput[DATA_WIDTH-1:0]AXI 写数据 数据
m_axi_wstrboutput[DATA_WIDTH/8-1:0]AXI 写数据 字节写使能
m_axi_wlastoutput1AXI 写数据 本burst最后数据拍
m_axi_wvalidoutput1AXI 写数据 有效，等待ready时保持
m_axi_wreadyinput1AXI 写数据 接收方就绪
m_axi_bidinput[ID_WIDTH-1:0]AXI 写响应 事务ID
m_axi_brespinput[1:0]AXI 写响应 响应码，OKAY=00
m_axi_bvalidinput1AXI 写响应 有效，等待ready时保持
m_axi_breadyoutput1AXI 写响应 接收方就绪

源文件：rtl/style_axi_arbiter.sv；下方内嵌当前源码，可直接在网页搜索。

展开完整源码
// Two AXI4 clients -> one DDR port. Client 0 is the least significant slice.
// At most one write and one read transaction outstanding, independently.
// Ownership is captured BEFORE offering AW/AR, and released on B/RLAST.
module style_axi_arbiter #(
    parameter integer ADDR_WIDTH = 32,
    parameter integer DATA_WIDTH = 128,
    parameter integer ID_WIDTH = 6,
    parameter integer READ_CLIENT1_LIMIT = 4
) (
    input wire clk, input wire rst_n,
    input wire [2*ID_WIDTH-1:0] s_axi_awid,
    input wire [2*ADDR_WIDTH-1:0] s_axi_awaddr,
    input wire [15:0] s_axi_awlen,
    input wire [5:0] s_axi_awsize,
    input wire [3:0] s_axi_awburst,
    input wire [1:0] s_axi_awvalid, output reg [1:0] s_axi_awready,
    input wire [2*DATA_WIDTH-1:0] s_axi_wdata,
    input wire [2*(DATA_WIDTH/8)-1:0] s_axi_wstrb,
    input wire [1:0] s_axi_wlast, input wire [1:0] s_axi_wvalid,
    output reg [1:0] s_axi_wready,
    output reg [2*ID_WIDTH-1:0] s_axi_bid,
    output reg [3:0] s_axi_bresp,
    output reg [1:0] s_axi_bvalid, input wire [1:0] s_axi_bready,
    input wire [2*ID_WIDTH-1:0] s_axi_arid,
    input wire [2*ADDR_WIDTH-1:0] s_axi_araddr,
    input wire [15:0] s_axi_arlen,
    input wire [5:0] s_axi_arsize,
    input wire [3:0] s_axi_arburst,
    input wire [1:0] s_axi_arvalid, output reg [1:0] s_axi_arready,
    output reg [2*ID_WIDTH-1:0] s_axi_rid,
    output reg [2*DATA_WIDTH-1:0] s_axi_rdata,
    output reg [3:0] s_axi_rresp,
    output reg [1:0] s_axi_rlast,
    output reg [1:0] s_axi_rvalid, input wire [1:0] s_axi_rready,
    output wire [ID_WIDTH-1:0] m_axi_awid,
    output wire [ADDR_WIDTH-1:0] m_axi_awaddr,
    output wire [7:0] m_axi_awlen,
    output wire [2:0] m_axi_awsize,
    output wire [1:0] m_axi_awburst,
    output wire m_axi_awvalid, input wire m_axi_awready,
    output wire [DATA_WIDTH-1:0] m_axi_wdata,
    output wire [DATA_WIDTH/8-1:0] m_axi_wstrb,
    output wire m_axi_wlast, output wire m_axi_wvalid,
    input wire m_axi_wready,
    input wire [ID_WIDTH-1:0] m_axi_bid,
    input wire [1:0] m_axi_bresp,
    input wire m_axi_bvalid, output wire m_axi_bready,
    output wire [ID_WIDTH-1:0] m_axi_arid,
    output wire [ADDR_WIDTH-1:0] m_axi_araddr,
    output wire [7:0] m_axi_arlen,
    output wire [2:0] m_axi_arsize,
    output wire [1:0] m_axi_arburst,
    output wire m_axi_arvalid, input wire m_axi_arready,
    input wire [ID_WIDTH-1:0] m_axi_rid,
    input wire [DATA_WIDTH-1:0] m_axi_rdata,
    input wire [1:0] m_axi_rresp,
    input wire m_axi_rlast, input wire m_axi_rvalid,
    output wire m_axi_rready
);
    reg wr_active, wr_owner, wr_next, aw_seen, w_seen;
    reg rd_active, rd_owner, ar_seen;
    reg [31:0] display_grants;
    assign m_axi_awid = s_axi_awid[wr_owner*ID_WIDTH +: ID_WIDTH];
    assign m_axi_awaddr = s_axi_awaddr[wr_owner*ADDR_WIDTH +: ADDR_WIDTH];
    assign m_axi_awlen = s_axi_awlen[wr_owner*8 +: 8];
    assign m_axi_awsize = s_axi_awsize[wr_owner*3 +: 3];
    assign m_axi_awburst = s_axi_awburst[wr_owner*2 +: 2];
    assign m_axi_awvalid = wr_active && !aw_seen && s_axi_awvalid[wr_owner];
    assign m_axi_wdata = s_axi_wdata[wr_owner*DATA_WIDTH +: DATA_WIDTH];
    assign m_axi_wstrb = s_axi_wstrb[wr_owner*(DATA_WIDTH/8) +: DATA_WIDTH/8];
    assign m_axi_wlast = s_axi_wlast[wr_owner];
    assign m_axi_wvalid = wr_active && !w_seen && s_axi_wvalid[wr_owner];
    assign m_axi_bready = wr_active && aw_seen && w_seen && s_axi_bready[wr_owner];
    assign m_axi_arid = s_axi_arid[rd_owner*ID_WIDTH +: ID_WIDTH];
    assign m_axi_araddr = s_axi_araddr[rd_owner*ADDR_WIDTH +: ADDR_WIDTH];
    assign m_axi_arlen = s_axi_arlen[rd_owner*8 +: 8];
    assign m_axi_arsize = s_axi_arsize[rd_owner*3 +: 3];
    assign m_axi_arburst = s_axi_arburst[rd_owner*2 +: 2];
    assign m_axi_arvalid = rd_active && !ar_seen && s_axi_arvalid[rd_owner];
    assign m_axi_rready = rd_active && ar_seen && s_axi_rready[rd_owner];

    always @* begin
        s_axi_awready = 0; s_axi_wready = 0;
        s_axi_bid = 0; s_axi_bresp = 0; s_axi_bvalid = 0;
        s_axi_arready = 0; s_axi_rid = 0; s_axi_rdata = 0;
        s_axi_rresp = 0; s_axi_rlast = 0; s_axi_rvalid = 0;
        if (wr_active) begin
            s_axi_awready[wr_owner] = !aw_seen && m_axi_awready;
            s_axi_wready[wr_owner] = !w_seen && m_axi_wready;
            s_axi_bid[wr_owner*ID_WIDTH +: ID_WIDTH] = m_axi_bid;
            s_axi_bresp[wr_owner*2 +: 2] = m_axi_bresp;
            s_axi_bvalid[wr_owner] = aw_seen && w_seen && m_axi_bvalid;
        end
        if (rd_active) begin
            s_axi_arready[rd_owner] = !ar_seen && m_axi_arready;
            s_axi_rid[rd_owner*ID_WIDTH +: ID_WIDTH] = m_axi_rid;
            s_axi_rdata[rd_owner*DATA_WIDTH +: DATA_WIDTH] = m_axi_rdata;
            s_axi_rresp[rd_owner*2 +: 2] = m_axi_rresp;
            s_axi_rlast[rd_owner] = ar_seen && m_axi_rlast;
            s_axi_rvalid[rd_owner] = ar_seen && m_axi_rvalid;
        end
    end

    always @(posedge clk) begin
        if (!rst_n) begin
            wr_active <= 0; wr_owner <= 0; wr_next <= 0;
            aw_seen <= 0; w_seen <= 0;
            rd_active <= 0; rd_owner <= 1; ar_seen <= 0; display_grants <= 0;
        end else begin
            if (!wr_active) begin
                if (s_axi_awvalid != 0) begin
                    wr_owner <= s_axi_awvalid == 2'b11 ? wr_next : s_axi_awvalid[1];
                    wr_active <= 1; aw_seen <= 0; w_seen <= 0;
                end
            end else begin
                if (m_axi_awvalid && m_axi_awready) aw_seen <= 1;
                if (m_axi_wvalid && m_axi_wready && m_axi_wlast) w_seen <= 1;
                if (m_axi_bvalid && m_axi_bready) begin
                    wr_active <= 0; wr_next <= !wr_owner;
                end
            end
            if (!rd_active) begin
                if (s_axi_arvalid != 0) begin
                    rd_owner <= s_axi_arvalid[1] &&
                        (!s_axi_arvalid[0] || display_grants < READ_CLIENT1_LIMIT);
                    rd_active <= 1; ar_seen <= 0;
                end
            end else begin
                if (m_axi_arvalid && m_axi_arready) ar_seen <= 1;
                if (m_axi_rvalid && m_axi_rready && m_axi_rlast) begin
                    rd_active <= 0;
                    if (!rd_owner) display_grants <= 0;
                    else if (display_grants < READ_CLIENT1_LIMIT)
                        display_grants <= display_grants + 1'b1;
                end
            end
        end
    end
endmodule
输入/结果帧池 style_frame_pool帧管理

维护输入与结果独立所有权，让原画和风格结果使用同一个 frame_id。

时钟域：核心 DDR 时钟域。复位极性以实际端口及下方源码为准。

实现逻辑

四个输入槽、两个结果槽。优先给采集一个空输入槽；计算选择最早 READY 输入与空结果。完成后保留原图和结果为 pending pair，显示安全换帧后才释放上一对。地址由 BASE_ADDR+slot*SLOT_BYTES 计算。

握手与时序

cap_take/cap_done、job_take/job_done、display_switch 是本域事件，必须与对应 slot 和成功标志同时提供。cap_done/job_done 只在全帧 DDR 响应确认后送入。

状态与流程

输入 FREE → CAPTURE → READY → PROCESS → RETAIN → FREE；结果 OUT_FREE → OUT_WRITE → OUT_RETAIN → OUT_FREE。

错误处理与恢复

失败采集和失败任务回收未发布槽；有待显示 pair 时不再取下一个 job，避免覆盖 pending。无空槽时上层显式丢新输入帧并计数。

实际参数

参数源码默认值
INPUT_SLOTS4
RESULT_SLOTS2
BASE_ADDR32'h01000000
SLOT_BYTES32'h00200000

全部实际端口

端口方向位宽声明用途与约定
clkinput1本模块核心/摄像头工作时钟，所属域见上文
rst_ninput1本域低有效复位

cap_doneinput1采集帧槽握手/地址信息：cap_done
cap_okinput1采集帧槽握手/地址信息：cap_ok
cap_take_slotinput[1:0]采集帧槽握手/地址信息：cap_take_slot
cap_done_slotinput[1:0]采集帧槽握手/地址信息：cap_done_slot

job_doneinput1任务输入/结果槽握手与配对信息：job_done

processed_countoutput[31:0]整帧结果DDR成功计数

error_countoutput[31:0]各阶段错误事件合计，非去重坏帧数

源文件：rtl/style_frame_pool.sv；下方内嵌当前源码，可直接在网页搜索。

展开完整源码
// Input and result pools have independent ownership. The displayed original is
// retained with its result until a safe display switch releases BOTH slots.
module style_frame_pool #(
    parameter integer INPUT_SLOTS=4, RESULT_SLOTS=2,
    parameter [31:0] BASE_ADDR=32'h01000000,
    parameter [31:0] SLOT_BYTES=32'h00200000
)(
    input wire clk,rst_n,
    output reg cap_available, output reg [1:0] cap_slot, output reg [31:0] cap_addr,
    input wire cap_take,cap_done,cap_ok,input wire [1:0] cap_take_slot,
    input wire [1:0] cap_done_slot,input wire [31:0] cap_done_id,
    output reg job_available,
    output reg [1:0] job_input_slot, output reg job_result_slot,
    output reg [31:0] job_input_addr,job_result_addr,job_frame_id,
    input wire job_take,job_done,job_ok,input wire [1:0] job_take_input,input wire job_take_result,
    input wire [1:0] job_done_input,input wire job_done_result,
    input wire display_switch,
    output reg front_valid,pending_valid,
    output reg [31:0] front_input_addr,front_result_addr,front_frame_id,
    output wire [3:0] ready_mask,
    output reg [31:0] captured_count,processed_count,displayed_count,error_count
);
    localparam [2:0] FREE=0,CAPTURE=1,READY=2,PROCESS=3,RETAIN=4;
    localparam [1:0] OUT_FREE=0,OUT_WRITE=1,OUT_RETAIN=2;
    reg [2:0] input_state [0:INPUT_SLOTS-1];
    reg [1:0] result_state [0:RESULT_SLOTS-1];
    reg [31:0] input_id [0:INPUT_SLOTS-1];
    reg [1:0] pending_input,front_input;
    reg pending_result,front_result;
    reg [31:0] pending_id;
    integer i;
    reg found_input,found_output;
    wire capture_failure=cap_done && input_state[cap_done_slot]==CAPTURE && !cap_ok;
    wire job_failure=job_done && input_state[job_done_input]==PROCESS &&
                     result_state[job_done_result]==OUT_WRITE && (!job_ok || pending_valid);
    always @* begin
        cap_available=0;cap_slot=0;cap_addr=BASE_ADDR;
        for(integer c=0;c<INPUT_SLOTS;c=c+1)
            if(!cap_available && input_state[c]==FREE) begin
                cap_available=1;cap_slot=c;cap_addr=BASE_ADDR+c*SLOT_BYTES;
            end
        found_input=0;found_output=0;
        job_input_slot=0;job_result_slot=0;job_frame_id=0;
        for(integer j=0;j<INPUT_SLOTS;j=j+1)
            if(input_state[j]==READY && (!found_input || $signed(input_id[j]-job_frame_id)<0)) begin
                found_input=1;job_input_slot=j;job_frame_id=input_id[j];
            end
        for(integer r=0;r<RESULT_SLOTS;r=r+1)
            if(!found_output && result_state[r]==OUT_FREE) begin found_output=1;job_result_slot=r;end
        job_available=found_input && found_output && !pending_valid;
        job_input_addr=BASE_ADDR+job_input_slot*SLOT_BYTES;
        job_result_addr=BASE_ADDR+(INPUT_SLOTS+job_result_slot)*SLOT_BYTES;
    end
    generate for(genvar k=0;k<4;k=k+1) begin:ready_bits
        if(k<INPUT_SLOTS) assign ready_mask[k]=input_state[k]==READY;
        else assign ready_mask[k]=1'b0;
    end endgenerate
    always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
        if(!rst_n) begin
            for(i=0;i<INPUT_SLOTS;i=i+1) begin input_state[i]<=FREE;input_id[i]<=0;end
            for(i=0;i<RESULT_SLOTS;i=i+1) result_state[i]<=OUT_FREE;
            front_valid<=0;pending_valid<=0;front_input<=0;front_result<=0;
            pending_input<=0;pending_result<=0;pending_id<=0;
            front_input_addr<=BASE_ADDR;front_result_addr<=BASE_ADDR+INPUT_SLOTS*SLOT_BYTES;
            front_frame_id<=0;captured_count<=0;processed_count<=0;displayed_count<=0;error_count<=0;
        end else begin
            // Independent capture and processing pipelines can fail together.
            // Use one assignment so neither increment overwrites the other.
            if(capture_failure || job_failure)
                error_count<=error_count+{31'b0,capture_failure}+{31'b0,job_failure};
            if(cap_take && input_state[cap_take_slot]==FREE) input_state[cap_take_slot]<=CAPTURE;
            if(cap_done && input_state[cap_done_slot]==CAPTURE) begin
                if(cap_ok) begin
                    input_state[cap_done_slot]<=READY;input_id[cap_done_slot]<=cap_done_id;
                    captured_count<=captured_count+1'b1;
                end else input_state[cap_done_slot]<=FREE;
            end
            if(job_take && input_state[job_take_input]==READY && result_state[job_take_result]==OUT_FREE) begin
                input_state[job_take_input]<=PROCESS;result_state[job_take_result]<=OUT_WRITE;
            end
            if(job_done && input_state[job_done_input]==PROCESS && result_state[job_done_result]==OUT_WRITE) begin
                if(job_ok && !pending_valid) begin
                    input_state[job_done_input]<=RETAIN;result_state[job_done_result]<=OUT_RETAIN;
                    pending_valid<=1;pending_input<=job_done_input;pending_result<=job_done_result;
                    pending_id<=input_id[job_done_input];processed_count<=processed_count+1'b1;
                end else begin
                    input_state[job_done_input]<=FREE;result_state[job_done_result]<=OUT_FREE;
                end
            end
            // Caller must first drain previous AXI reads AND flush display FIFO
            // while the pixel domain is in its safe window.
            if(display_switch && pending_valid) begin
                if(front_valid) begin input_state[front_input]<=FREE;result_state[front_result]<=OUT_FREE;end
                front_valid<=1;front_input<=pending_input;front_result<=pending_result;
                front_input_addr<=BASE_ADDR+pending_input*SLOT_BYTES;
                front_result_addr<=BASE_ADDR+(INPUT_SLOTS+pending_result)*SLOT_BYTES;
                front_frame_id<=pending_id;pending_valid<=0;displayed_count<=displayed_count+1'b1;
            end
        end
    end
endmodule
复制占位计算模块 style_accel_bypass队友接口

队友 CNN 未完成时先验证完整任务链路，输入像素和标志原样返回。

时钟域：核心 DDR 时钟域。复位极性以实际端口及下方源码为准。

实现逻辑

一个弹性寄存器保存 RGB、SOF/EOL/EOF 和 frame_id。它没有卷积、网络权重或风格迁移能力。

握手与时序

标准 ready/valid，空输出或下游愿意接收时可接新数据；输出等待时 payload 保持。

状态与流程

valid 一位状态，数据打包为 59 位。

错误处理与恢复

clear 取消本地占位输出，任务取消仍由平台处理 DMA 和帧槽。

实际参数

无可配置模块参数。

全部实际端口

端口方向位宽声明用途与约定
clkinput1本模块核心/摄像头工作时钟，所属域见上文
rst_ninput1本域低有效复位
clearinput1取消本地缓存内容，高有效；上层须先保证事务安全
s_rgbinput[23:0]输入流：RGB888像素
s_validinput1输入流：有效，握手才推进
s_sofinput1输入流：第一像素标志
s_eolinput1输入流：行尾像素标志
s_eofinput1输入流：帧尾像素标志
s_frame_idinput[31:0]输入流：源帧ID
s_readyoutput1输入流：接收就绪
m_rgboutput[23:0]输出流：RGB888像素
m_validoutput1输出流：有效，握手才推进
m_sofoutput1输出流：第一像素标志
m_eoloutput1输出流：行尾像素标志
m_eofoutput1输出流：帧尾像素标志
m_frame_idoutput[31:0]输出流：源帧ID
m_readyinput1输出流：接收就绪

源文件：rtl/style_accel_bypass.sv；下方内嵌当前源码，可直接在网页搜索。

展开完整源码
// Stand-in for the teammate's complete RGB888-in/RGB888-out processing block.
// This copies pixels: it is neither a CNN nor proof of neural style transfer.
module style_accel_bypass(
    input wire clk,rst_n,clear,
    input wire [23:0] s_rgb,input wire s_valid,s_sof,s_eol,s_eof,
    input wire [31:0] s_frame_id,output wire s_ready,
    output wire [23:0] m_rgb,output wire m_valid,m_sof,m_eol,m_eof,
    output wire [31:0] m_frame_id,input wire m_ready
);
    reg [58:0] payload;
    reg valid;
    assign s_ready=!valid || m_ready;
    assign m_valid=valid;
    assign {m_frame_id,m_eof,m_eol,m_sof,m_rgb}=payload;
    always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
        if(!rst_n) begin valid<=0;payload<=0;end
        else if(clear) valid<=0;
        else if(s_ready) begin
            valid<=s_valid;
            if(s_valid) payload<={s_frame_id,s_eof,s_eol,s_sof,s_rgb};
        end
    end
endmodule
平台控制与集成核心 style_platform_core集成

连接采集、帧池、复制/外部加速器、显示、四个 DMA、AXI 仲裁和 APB 寄存器。

时钟域：核心 DDR 时钟域。复位极性以实际端口及下方源码为准。

实现逻辑

采集FSM逐像素核对帧编号及SOF/EOL/EOF；任务FSM读READY输入并写结果；显示FSM排空旧读、清FIFO、在安全窗口切换，再逐行原图640像素/结果640像素。READ_BURST_BEATS默认128用于任务/显示读，写DMA仍16拍。FORCE_BYPASS=1只复制，未实例化Sapphire。

握手与时序

AXI128、ID_WIDTH=4；采集写 ID0，结果写 ID1，计算读 ID2，显示读 ID3。外部加速器是 RGB888 双向流及 accel_start/accel_abort，帧标志只在握手拍有效。start之后有J_ARM空拍，允许队友按时钟启动并清除旧valid；abort期间队友必须清空未交付输出并回到空闲。APB3 是 offsets 接口，不包含 CPU 系统基地址。

状态与流程

采集 C_IDLE/C_COMMAND/C_STREAM/C_WAIT/C_ABORT；任务 J_IDLE/J_COMMAND/J_ARM/J_STREAM/J_WAIT/J_ABORT；显示 D_WAIT/D_DRAIN/D_FLUSH/D_SETTLE/D_COMMAND/D_STREAM。

错误处理与恢复

坏 frame marker、摄像头 abort、DMA 错误都禁止发布半帧；sticky fault 形成 fatal_fault 并停止新任务，需系统复位。显示欠载计数变化时立即进入D_DRAIN、取消旧读并丢弃本地解包数据，避免像素端标粉后停止消费造成超过10ms的无进展误超时。旧AXI仍按协议排空；FIFO清空和pair释放继续等safe_window。错误/丢帧合计是各阶段事件之和，同一坏帧可被多阶段记录，不是去重后的坏帧数。

实际参数

参数源码默认值
WIDTH640
HEIGHT480
ID_WIDTH4
CORE_HZ100000000
FORCE_BYPASS1
READ_BURST_BEATS128
PERF_ENABLE0

// Generated by scripts/generate_core.py. Platform-only logic; no CPU/CNN is hidden here.
module style_platform_core #(
    parameter integer WIDTH=640,HEIGHT=480,ID_WIDTH=4,CORE_HZ=100000000,
    parameter integer FORCE_BYPASS=1,READ_BURST_BEATS=128,PERF_ENABLE=0
)(
    input wire clk,rst_n,cal_ready,
    input wire cam_valid,cam_sof,cam_eol,cam_eof,input wire [23:0] cam_rgb,
    input wire [31:0] cam_frame_id,output wire cam_ready,
    input wire cam_abort,input wire [31:0] cam_abort_id,
    input wire [31:0] camera_frames,camera_drops,camera_errors,
    input wire display_refresh_request,display_safe_window,
    output reg display_refresh_ack,display_flush,
    output wire [23:0] display_rgb,output wire display_valid,input wire display_ready,
    output wire front_valid,output wire [31:0] front_frame_id,
    output wire [1:0] display_mode,output wire [15:0] capture_fps,process_fps,
    input wire [31:0] underflow_count,output reg bypass_active,
    input wire [11:0] paddr,input wire psel,penable,pwrite,input wire [31:0] pwdata,
    output wire [31:0] prdata,output wire pready,pslverr,irq,
    output wire [23:0] accel_input_rgb,output wire accel_input_valid,
    input wire accel_input_ready,output wire accel_input_sof,accel_input_eol,accel_input_eof,
    output wire [31:0] accel_input_frame_id,
    input wire [23:0] accel_output_rgb,input wire accel_output_valid,
    output wire accel_output_ready,input wire accel_output_sof,accel_output_eol,accel_output_eof,
    input wire [31:0] accel_output_frame_id,output reg accel_start,output wire accel_abort,
    output wire [31:0] captured_count,processed_count,displayed_count,
    output wire [31:0] drop_count,error_count,output wire fatal_fault,
    input wire [15:0] perf_camera_level,perf_display_level,
    output wire [1023:0] perf_snapshot,output wire perf_snapshot_valid,
    output wire [ID_WIDTH-1:0] m_axi_awid,
    output wire [32-1:0] m_axi_awaddr,
    output wire [8-1:0] m_axi_awlen,
    output wire [3-1:0] m_axi_awsize,
    output wire [2-1:0] m_axi_awburst,
    output wire m_axi_awvalid,
    input wire m_axi_awready,
    output wire [128-1:0] m_axi_wdata,
    output wire [16-1:0] m_axi_wstrb,
    output wire m_axi_wlast,
    output wire m_axi_wvalid,
    input wire m_axi_wready,
    input wire [ID_WIDTH-1:0] m_axi_bid,
    input wire [2-1:0] m_axi_bresp,
    input wire m_axi_bvalid,
    output wire m_axi_bready,
    output wire [ID_WIDTH-1:0] m_axi_arid,
    output wire [32-1:0] m_axi_araddr,
    output wire [8-1:0] m_axi_arlen,
    output wire [3-1:0] m_axi_arsize,
    output wire [2-1:0] m_axi_arburst,
    output wire m_axi_arvalid,
    input wire m_axi_arready,
    input wire [ID_WIDTH-1:0] m_axi_rid,
    input wire [128-1:0] m_axi_rdata,
    input wire [2-1:0] m_axi_rresp,
    input wire m_axi_rlast,
    input wire m_axi_rvalid,
    output wire m_axi_rready);

wire [ID_WIDTH-1:0] cw_awid;
wire [32-1:0] cw_awaddr;
wire [8-1:0] cw_awlen;
wire [3-1:0] cw_awsize;

wire cw_wvalid;

wire [32-1:0] pw_awaddr;
wire [8-1:0] pw_awlen;
wire [3-1:0] pw_awsize;
wire [2-1:0] pw_awburst;
wire pw_awvalid;

wire [128-1:0] pw_wdata;
wire [16-1:0] pw_wstrb;
wire pw_wlast;
