wire pw_wready;
wire [ID_WIDTH-1:0] pw_bid;
wire [2-1:0] pw_bresp;
wire pw_bvalid;
wire pw_bready;

wire [ID_WIDTH-1:0] pr_arid;
wire [32-1:0] pr_araddr;
wire [8-1:0] pr_arlen;
wire [3-1:0] pr_arsize;
wire [2-1:0] pr_arburst;
wire pr_arvalid;
wire pr_arready;
wire [ID_WIDTH-1:0] pr_rid;
wire [128-1:0] pr_rdata;
wire [2-1:0] pr_rresp;
wire pr_rlast;
wire pr_rvalid;
wire pr_rready;

wire [ID_WIDTH-1:0] dr_arid;
wire [32-1:0] dr_araddr;
wire [8-1:0] dr_arlen;
wire [3-1:0] dr_arsize;
wire [2-1:0] dr_arburst;
wire dr_arvalid;
wire dr_arready;
wire [ID_WIDTH-1:0] dr_rid;
wire [128-1:0] dr_rdata;
wire [2-1:0] dr_rresp;
wire dr_rlast;
wire dr_rvalid;
wire dr_rready;

    localparam integer PIXELS=WIDTH*HEIGHT, BEATS=PIXELS/4;
    wire enabled,auto_run,cfg_bypass,start_pulse,pending_valid;
    wire cap_available,job_available;
    wire [1:0] cap_slot,job_input_slot;
    wire job_result_slot;
    wire [31:0] cap_addr,job_input_addr,job_result_addr,job_frame_id;
    wire [31:0] front_input_addr,front_result_addr,pool_errors;
    wire [3:0] ready_mask;
    reg cap_take,cap_done,cap_ok,job_take,job_done,job_ok,display_switch;
    reg [1:0] captured_slot,work_input;
    reg work_result;
    reg [31:0] captured_id,work_id;
    reg [31:0] capture_drop_count,protocol_error_count,display_error_count;
    assign drop_count=camera_drops+capture_drop_count;
    assign error_count=camera_errors+pool_errors+protocol_error_count+display_error_count;
    style_frame_pool pool(
        .clk(clk),.rst_n(rst_n),.cap_available(cap_available),.cap_slot(cap_slot),.cap_addr(cap_addr),
        .cap_take(cap_take),.cap_take_slot(captured_slot),.cap_done(cap_done),.cap_ok(cap_ok),.cap_done_slot(captured_slot),.cap_done_id(captured_id),
        .job_available(job_available),.job_input_slot(job_input_slot),.job_result_slot(job_result_slot),
        .job_input_addr(job_input_addr),.job_result_addr(job_result_addr),.job_frame_id(job_frame_id),
        .job_take(job_take),.job_take_input(work_input),.job_take_result(work_result),.job_done(job_done),.job_ok(job_ok),.job_done_input(work_input),.job_done_result(work_result),
        .display_switch(display_switch),.front_valid(front_valid),.pending_valid(pending_valid),
        .front_input_addr(front_input_addr),.front_result_addr(front_result_addr),.front_frame_id(front_frame_id),
        .ready_mask(ready_mask),.captured_count(captured_count),.processed_count(processed_count),
        .displayed_count(displayed_count),.error_count(pool_errors)
    );
    reg [31:0] second_count;
    wire fps_tick=second_count==CORE_HZ-1;
    always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n)
        if(!rst_n) second_count<=0;
        else if(fps_tick) second_count<=0;else second_count<=second_count+1'b1;
    reg manual_pending,fatal_previous;
    always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
        if(!rst_n) begin manual_pending<=0;fatal_previous<=0;end
        else begin
            fatal_previous<=fatal_fault;
            if(job_take) manual_pending<=0;
            // A command issued before a captured frame exists is retained.
            // Multiple commands coalesce into this one queued request.
            if(start_pulse) manual_pending<=1;
        end
    end
    wire fault_event=(fatal_fault && !fatal_previous) || cw_error || pw_error || pr_error || dr_error;
    wire [31:0] status={20'b0,manual_pending,bypass_active,4'b0,fatal_fault,pending_valid,front_valid,
                       (jstate!=J_IDLE),(cstate!=C_IDLE),cal_ready};
    style_control_regs #(.WIDTH(WIDTH),.HEIGHT(HEIGHT)) regs(
        .clk(clk),.rst_n(rst_n),.paddr(paddr),.psel(psel && !perf_select),.penable(penable),.pwrite(pwrite),.pwdata(pwdata),
        .prdata(regs_prdata),.pready(regs_pready),.pslverr(regs_pslverr),.enable(enabled),.auto_run(auto_run),.bypass(cfg_bypass),
        .display_mode(display_mode),.start_pulse(start_pulse),.status(status),.camera_frames(camera_frames),
        .captured_count(captured_count),.processed_count(processed_count),.displayed_count(displayed_count),
        .drop_count(drop_count),.error_count(error_count),.underflow_count(underflow_count),.front_id(front_frame_id),
        .ready_mask(ready_mask),.event_set({cap_done&&!cap_ok || job_done&&!job_ok || fault_event,
             display_switch && pending_valid,job_done && job_ok,cap_done && cap_ok}),
        .irq(irq),.capture_fps(capture_fps),.process_fps(process_fps),.fps_tick(fps_tick)
    );

    // Capture state machine validates EVERY pixel and every frame marker.
    localparam [2:0] C_IDLE=0,C_COMMAND=1,C_STREAM=2,C_WAIT=3,C_ABORT=4;
    reg [2:0] cstate;
    reg [31:0] capture_addr,capture_pixels,bad_camera_id;
    reg bad_camera_id_valid;
    reg [15:0] capture_x,capture_y;
    wire cw_cmd_ready,cw_busy,cw_done,cw_error,cw_fault;
    wire [7:0] cw_code;
    wire cap_pack_ready,cap_pack_valid,cap_pack_last,cw_stream_ready;
    wire [127:0] cap_pack_data;
    wire capture_marker_bad=cam_frame_id!=captured_id || cam_sof!=(capture_pixels==0) ||
         cam_eol!=(capture_x==WIDTH-1) || cam_eof!=(capture_pixels==PIXELS-1);
    wire capture_abort_match=((cam_abort && cam_abort_id==captured_id) ||
          (bad_camera_id_valid && bad_camera_id==captured_id)) && cstate!=C_IDLE;
    wire capture_bad=capture_marker_bad || capture_abort_match;
    assign cam_ready=cstate==C_IDLE ? !(cam_valid && cam_sof && enabled && cal_ready && cap_available &&
                      !(bad_camera_id_valid && cam_frame_id==bad_camera_id) && !fatal_fault) :
                     (cstate==C_STREAM ? (capture_bad ? 1'b0:cap_pack_ready):1'b0);
    style_pixel_packer capture_pack(
        .clk(clk),.rst_n(rst_n),.clear(cstate==C_IDLE || cstate==C_ABORT),
        .s_rgb(cam_rgb),.s_valid(cam_valid && cstate==C_STREAM && !capture_bad),.s_last(cam_eof),
        .s_ready(cap_pack_ready),.m_data(cap_pack_data),.m_valid(cap_pack_valid),.m_last(cap_pack_last),.m_ready(cw_stream_ready)
    );
    always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
        if(!rst_n) begin
            cstate<=C_IDLE;cap_take<=0;cap_done<=0;cap_ok<=0;captured_slot<=0;captured_id<=0;
            capture_addr<=0;capture_pixels<=0;capture_x<=0;capture_y<=0;capture_drop_count<=0;
            bad_camera_id<=0;bad_camera_id_valid<=0;
        end else begin
            cap_take<=0;cap_done<=0;
            if(cam_abort) begin bad_camera_id<=cam_abort_id;bad_camera_id_valid<=1;end
            case(cstate)
                C_IDLE:if(cam_valid && cam_sof) begin
                    if(enabled && cal_ready && cap_available && !fatal_fault &&
                       !(bad_camera_id_valid && cam_frame_id==bad_camera_id) &&
                       !(cam_abort && cam_abort_id==cam_frame_id)) begin
                        cap_take<=1;captured_slot<=cap_slot;captured_id<=cam_frame_id;capture_addr<=cap_addr;
                        capture_pixels<=0;capture_x<=0;capture_y<=0;cstate<=C_COMMAND;
                    end else if(cam_ready) capture_drop_count<=capture_drop_count+1'b1;
                end
                C_COMMAND:if(cw_cmd_ready) cstate<=C_STREAM;
                C_STREAM:begin
                    if((cam_valid && capture_marker_bad) || capture_abort_match) cstate<=C_ABORT;
                    else if(cam_valid && cam_ready) begin
                        capture_pixels<=capture_pixels+1'b1;
                        if(capture_x==WIDTH-1) begin capture_x<=0;capture_y<=capture_y+1'b1;end
                        else capture_x<=capture_x+1'b1;
                        if(cam_eof) cstate<=C_WAIT;
                    end
                    if(cw_fault) cstate<=C_ABORT;
                    else if(cw_done) begin cap_done<=1;cap_ok<=0;cstate<=C_IDLE;end
                end
                C_WAIT:if(cw_fault) cstate<=C_ABORT;
                else if(cw_done) begin
                    cap_done<=1;cap_ok<=!cw_error && capture_pixels==PIXELS;cstate<=C_IDLE;
                end else if(capture_abort_match) cstate<=C_ABORT;
                // A fatal bus timeout may still own an accepted AXI request.
                // Retain its slot until coordinated reset, never recycle it.
                C_ABORT:if(!cw_fault && (cw_done || !cw_busy)) begin cap_done<=1;cap_ok<=0;cstate<=C_IDLE;end
                default:cstate<=C_IDLE;
            endcase
        end
    end

    // Image reader -> teammate stream (or copy stub) -> result writer.
    localparam [2:0] J_IDLE=0,J_COMMAND=1,J_STREAM=2,J_WAIT=3,J_ABORT=4,J_ARM=5;
    reg [2:0] jstate;
    reg [31:0] work_read_addr,work_write_addr,input_pixels,output_pixels;
    reg [15:0] input_x,input_y,output_x,output_y;
    reg read_commanded,write_commanded,read_finished,write_finished,work_bypass;
    reg pending_bypass;
    wire pr_cmd_ready,pr_busy,pr_done,pr_error,pr_fault;
    wire pw_cmd_ready,pw_busy,pw_done,pw_error,pw_fault;
    wire [7:0] pr_code,pw_code;
    wire [127:0] pr_data;
    wire pr_valid,pr_last,pr_stream_ready;
    wire [23:0] input_rgb,copy_rgb,output_rgb;
    wire input_valid,input_last,input_ready,copy_valid,copy_sof,copy_eol,copy_eof;
    wire [31:0] copy_id;
    wire output_valid,output_sof,output_eol,output_eof,output_ready;
    wire [31:0] output_id;
    wire result_pack_ready,result_pack_valid,result_pack_last,pw_stream_ready;
    wire [127:0] result_pack_data;
    wire output_bad=output_id!=work_id || output_sof!=(output_pixels==0) ||
         output_eol!=(output_x==WIDTH-1) || output_eof!=(output_pixels==PIXELS-1);
    style_pixel_unpacker job_unpack(
        .clk(clk),.rst_n(rst_n),.clear(jstate==J_IDLE || jstate==J_ABORT),
        .s_data(pr_data),.s_valid(pr_valid),.s_last(pr_last),.s_ready(pr_stream_ready),
        .m_rgb(input_rgb),.m_valid(input_valid),.m_last(input_last),.m_ready(input_ready)
    );
    assign accel_input_rgb=input_rgb;
    assign accel_input_valid=input_valid && jstate==J_STREAM && !work_bypass;
    assign accel_input_sof=input_pixels==0;assign accel_input_eol=input_x==WIDTH-1;
    assign accel_input_eof=input_pixels==PIXELS-1;assign accel_input_frame_id=work_id;
    wire copy_input_ready;
    assign input_ready=jstate==J_STREAM && (work_bypass ? copy_input_ready:accel_input_ready);
    style_accel_bypass copy_stub(
        .clk(clk),.rst_n(rst_n),.clear(jstate==J_IDLE || jstate==J_ABORT),.s_rgb(input_rgb),
        .s_valid(input_valid && jstate==J_STREAM && work_bypass),.s_ready(copy_input_ready),
        .s_sof(input_pixels==0),.s_eol(input_x==WIDTH-1),.s_eof(input_pixels==PIXELS-1),.s_frame_id(work_id),
        .m_rgb(copy_rgb),.m_valid(copy_valid),.m_ready(output_ready && work_bypass),
        .m_sof(copy_sof),.m_eol(copy_eol),.m_eof(copy_eof),.m_frame_id(copy_id)
    );
    assign output_rgb=work_bypass?copy_rgb:accel_output_rgb;
    assign output_valid=work_bypass?copy_valid:accel_output_valid;
    assign output_sof=work_bypass?copy_sof:accel_output_sof;
    assign output_eol=work_bypass?copy_eol:accel_output_eol;
    assign output_eof=work_bypass?copy_eof:accel_output_eof;
    assign output_id=work_bypass?copy_id:accel_output_frame_id;
    assign output_ready=jstate==J_STREAM && result_pack_ready;
    assign accel_output_ready=output_ready && !work_bypass;
    // Abort is held until the DDR commands have drained. The accelerator must
    // clear its pending output/pipeline and remain idle until the next start.
    assign accel_abort=jstate==J_ABORT;
    style_pixel_packer result_pack(
        .clk(clk),.rst_n(rst_n),.clear(jstate==J_IDLE || jstate==J_ABORT),
        .s_rgb(output_rgb),.s_valid(output_valid && jstate==J_STREAM && !output_bad),.s_last(output_eof),
        .s_ready(result_pack_ready),.m_data(result_pack_data),.m_valid(result_pack_valid),
        .m_last(result_pack_last),.m_ready(pw_stream_ready)
    );
    always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
        if(!rst_n) begin
            jstate<=J_IDLE;job_take<=0;job_done<=0;job_ok<=0;work_input<=0;work_result<=0;work_id<=0;
            work_read_addr<=0;work_write_addr<=0;input_pixels<=0;output_pixels<=0;
            input_x<=0;input_y<=0;output_x<=0;output_y<=0;
            read_commanded<=0;write_commanded<=0;read_finished<=0;write_finished<=0;
            work_bypass<=1;pending_bypass<=1;accel_start<=0;protocol_error_count<=0;
        end else begin
            job_take<=0;job_done<=0;accel_start<=0;
            if(pr_done) read_finished<=1;
            if(pw_done) write_finished<=1;
            case(jstate)
                J_IDLE:if(enabled && cal_ready && job_available && !job_done && (auto_run || manual_pending || start_pulse) && !fatal_fault) begin
                    job_take<=1;work_input<=job_input_slot;work_result<=job_result_slot;work_id<=job_frame_id;
                    work_read_addr<=job_input_addr;work_write_addr<=job_result_addr;
                    work_bypass<=FORCE_BYPASS || cfg_bypass;
                    input_pixels<=0;output_pixels<=0;input_x<=0;input_y<=0;output_x<=0;output_y<=0;
                    read_commanded<=0;write_commanded<=0;read_finished<=0;write_finished<=0;jstate<=J_COMMAND;
                end
                J_COMMAND:begin
                    if(pr_cmd_ready && !read_commanded) read_commanded<=1;
                    if(pw_cmd_ready && !write_commanded) write_commanded<=1;
                    if((read_commanded || pr_cmd_ready) && (write_commanded || pw_cmd_ready)) begin
                        jstate<=J_ARM;accel_start<=1;
                    end
                end
                // Give a clocked accelerator one edge to consume start and
                // clear an old held output before exposing ready/valid.
                J_ARM:jstate<=J_STREAM;
                J_STREAM:begin
                    if(input_valid && input_ready) begin
                        input_pixels<=input_pixels+1'b1;
                        if(input_x==WIDTH-1) begin input_x<=0;input_y<=input_y+1'b1;end else input_x<=input_x+1'b1;
                    end
                    if(output_valid && output_ready) begin
                        if(output_bad) begin jstate<=J_ABORT;protocol_error_count<=protocol_error_count+1'b1;end
                        else begin
                            output_pixels<=output_pixels+1'b1;
                            if(output_x==WIDTH-1) begin output_x<=0;output_y<=output_y+1'b1;end else output_x<=output_x+1'b1;
                            if(output_eof) begin
                                if(input_pixels + (input_valid && input_ready) != PIXELS) begin
                                    jstate<=J_ABORT;protocol_error_count<=protocol_error_count+1'b1;
                                end else jstate<=J_WAIT;
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if(pr_error || pw_error || pr_fault || pw_fault) jstate<=J_ABORT;
                end
                J_WAIT:begin
                    if(pr_error || pw_error || pr_fault || pw_fault) jstate<=J_ABORT;
                    else if((read_finished || pr_done) && (write_finished || pw_done)) begin
                        job_done<=1;job_ok<=input_pixels==PIXELS && output_pixels==PIXELS;
                        pending_bypass<=work_bypass;jstate<=J_IDLE;
                    end
                end
                J_ABORT:if(!pr_busy && !pw_busy && !pr_fault && !pw_fault) begin
                    job_done<=1;job_ok<=0;jstate<=J_IDLE;
                end
                default:jstate<=J_IDLE;
            endcase
        end
    end

    // Display fetches each row's original half, then result half. The stream
    // contains exactly 2*WIDTH*HEIGHT pixels; video blanking is never stored.
    localparam [2:0] D_WAIT=0,D_DRAIN=1,D_FLUSH=2,D_SETTLE=3,D_COMMAND=4,D_STREAM=5;
    reg [2:0] dstate;
    (* async_reg="true" *) reg [1:0] refresh_sync,safe_sync;
    reg [6:0] flush_count;
    reg [15:0] display_row;
    reg display_half;
    reg [31:0] display_input_addr,display_result_addr;
    reg [31:0] underflow_seen;
    wire dr_cmd_ready,dr_busy,dr_done,dr_error,dr_fault;
    wire [7:0] dr_code;
    wire [127:0] dr_data;
    wire dr_valid,dr_last,dr_stream_ready,display_unpack_last;
    wire discard_display=dstate==D_DRAIN || dstate==D_FLUSH || dstate==D_SETTLE;
    assign display_valid=display_unpack_valid && !discard_display;
    wire display_unpack_valid;
    style_pixel_unpacker display_unpack(
        .clk(clk),.rst_n(rst_n),.clear(discard_display),
        .s_data(dr_data),.s_valid(dr_valid),.s_last(dr_last),.s_ready(dr_stream_ready),
        .m_rgb(display_rgb),.m_valid(display_unpack_valid),.m_last(display_unpack_last),.m_ready(display_ready && !discard_display)
    );
    always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
        if(!rst_n) begin
            refresh_sync<=0;safe_sync<=0;dstate<=D_WAIT;display_refresh_ack<=0;display_flush<=0;
            display_switch<=0;flush_count<=0;display_row<=0;display_half<=0;
            display_input_addr<=0;display_result_addr<=0;display_error_count<=0;bypass_active<=1;underflow_seen<=0;
        end else begin
            refresh_sync<={refresh_sync[0],display_refresh_request};safe_sync<={safe_sync[0],display_safe_window};
            display_switch<=0;
            underflow_seen<=underflow_count;
            if(!refresh_sync[1]) display_refresh_ack<=0;
            if(dr_error) display_error_count<=display_error_count+1'b1;
            // A poisoned video frame stops FIFO consumption. Abort/drain its
            // outstanding read immediately rather than waiting up to 16.7 ms
            // for refresh; otherwise legitimate backpressure can hit watchdog.
            // Ownership and FIFO reset still wait for the safe window below.
            if((refresh_sync[1] && !display_refresh_ack || underflow_count!=underflow_seen) &&
               dstate!=D_DRAIN && dstate!=D_FLUSH && dstate!=D_SETTLE)
                dstate<=D_DRAIN;
            else case(dstate)
                D_WAIT:;
                D_DRAIN:if(!dr_busy && !dr_fault && safe_sync[1]) begin
                    display_flush<=1;flush_count<=0;display_switch<=1;
                    if(pending_valid) bypass_active<=pending_bypass;
                    dstate<=D_FLUSH;
                end
                D_FLUSH:if(flush_count==15) begin display_flush<=0;flush_count<=0;dstate<=D_SETTLE;end
                    else flush_count<=flush_count+1'b1;
                D_SETTLE:if(flush_count==63) begin
                    // Pair is now stable, both FIFO domains have left reset.
                    // ACK is held until the pixel-domain request is withdrawn.
                    if(safe_sync[1]) begin
                        display_input_addr<=front_input_addr;display_result_addr<=front_result_addr;
                        display_row<=0;display_half<=0;display_refresh_ack<=1;
                        dstate<=front_valid?D_COMMAND:D_WAIT;
                    end
                end else flush_count<=flush_count+1'b1;
                D_COMMAND:if(dr_cmd_ready && !refresh_sync[1]) dstate<=D_STREAM;
                D_STREAM:if(dr_done) begin
                    if(dr_error) dstate<=D_WAIT;
                    else if(!display_half) begin display_half<=1;dstate<=D_COMMAND;end
                    else if(display_row==HEIGHT-1) dstate<=D_WAIT;
                    else begin display_half<=0;display_row<=display_row+1'b1;dstate<=D_COMMAND;end
                end
                default:dstate<=D_WAIT;
            endcase
        end
    end
    assign fatal_fault=cw_fault || pw_fault || pr_fault || dr_fault;

// Passive instrumentation bank 0x100..0x17f, enabled explicitly by the shell.
wire [31:0] regs_prdata,perf_prdata;
wire regs_pready,regs_pslverr,perf_pslverr;
wire perf_select=PERF_ENABLE && paddr[11:7]==5'd2;
assign prdata=perf_select?perf_prdata:regs_prdata;
assign pready=perf_select?1'b1:regs_pready;
assign pslverr=perf_select?perf_pslverr:regs_pslverr;
generate if(PERF_ENABLE) begin:performance
    style_perf_monitor #(.CORE_HZ(CORE_HZ)) monitor(
        .clk(clk),.rst_n(rst_n),.second_tick(fps_tick),
        .cap_start(cap_take),.cap_done(cap_done),.cap_ok(cap_ok),.cap_slot(captured_slot),.cap_id(captured_id),
        .job_start(job_take),.job_done(job_done),.job_ok(job_ok),.job_slot(work_input),.job_id(work_id),
        .publish(display_switch && pending_valid),
        .arvalid(m_axi_arvalid),.arready(m_axi_arready),.rvalid(m_axi_rvalid),.rready(m_axi_rready),.rlast(m_axi_rlast),
        .awvalid(m_axi_awvalid),.awready(m_axi_awready),.wvalid(m_axi_wvalid),.wready(m_axi_wready),
        .wlast(m_axi_wlast),.wstrb(m_axi_wstrb),.bvalid(m_axi_bvalid),.bready(m_axi_bready),
        .camera_level(perf_camera_level),.display_level(perf_display_level),
        .paddr(paddr[6:0]),.psel(psel && perf_select),.penable(penable),.pwrite(pwrite),.pwdata(pwdata),
        .prdata(perf_prdata),.pslverr(perf_pslverr),.snapshot(perf_snapshot),.snapshot_valid(perf_snapshot_valid));
end else begin:no_performance
    assign perf_prdata=0;assign perf_pslverr=0;assign perf_snapshot=0;assign perf_snapshot_valid=0;
end endgenerate


style_axi_write_dma #(.ID_WIDTH(ID_WIDTH),.AXI_ID(0)) cw_dma(
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .cmd_valid(cstate==C_COMMAND),
    .cmd_ready(cw_cmd_ready),
    .cmd_addr(capture_addr),
    .cmd_beats(BEATS),
    .abort(cstate==C_ABORT),
    .busy(cw_busy),
    .done(cw_done),
    .error(cw_error),
    .error_code(cw_code),
    .sticky_fault(cw_fault),
    .s_data(cap_pack_data),
    .s_valid(cap_pack_valid),
    .s_ready(cw_stream_ready),
    .s_last(cap_pack_last),
    .m_axi_awid(cw_awid),
    .m_axi_awaddr(cw_awaddr),
    .m_axi_awlen(cw_awlen),
    .m_axi_awsize(cw_awsize),
    .m_axi_awburst(cw_awburst),
    .m_axi_awvalid(cw_awvalid),
    .m_axi_awready(cw_awready),
    .m_axi_wdata(cw_wdata),
    .m_axi_wstrb(cw_wstrb),
    .m_axi_wlast(cw_wlast),
    .m_axi_wvalid(cw_wvalid),
    .m_axi_wready(cw_wready),
    .m_axi_bid(cw_bid),
    .m_axi_bresp(cw_bresp),
    .m_axi_bvalid(cw_bvalid),
    .m_axi_bready(cw_bready)
);

style_axi_write_dma #(.ID_WIDTH(ID_WIDTH),.AXI_ID(1)) pw_dma(
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .cmd_valid(jstate==J_COMMAND && !write_commanded),
    .cmd_ready(pw_cmd_ready),
    .cmd_addr(work_write_addr),
    .cmd_beats(BEATS),
    .abort(jstate==J_ABORT),
    .busy(pw_busy),
    .done(pw_done),
    .error(pw_error),
    .error_code(pw_code),
    .sticky_fault(pw_fault),
    .s_data(result_pack_data),
    .s_valid(result_pack_valid),
    .s_ready(pw_stream_ready),
    .s_last(result_pack_last),
    .m_axi_awid(pw_awid),
    .m_axi_awaddr(pw_awaddr),
    .m_axi_awlen(pw_awlen),
    .m_axi_awsize(pw_awsize),
    .m_axi_awburst(pw_awburst),
    .m_axi_awvalid(pw_awvalid),
    .m_axi_awready(pw_awready),
    .m_axi_wdata(pw_wdata),
    .m_axi_wstrb(pw_wstrb),
    .m_axi_wlast(pw_wlast),
    .m_axi_wvalid(pw_wvalid),
    .m_axi_wready(pw_wready),
    .m_axi_bid(pw_bid),
    .m_axi_bresp(pw_bresp),
    .m_axi_bvalid(pw_bvalid),
    .m_axi_bready(pw_bready)
);

style_axi_read_dma #(.ID_WIDTH(ID_WIDTH),.AXI_ID(2),.BURST_BEATS(READ_BURST_BEATS)) pr_dma(
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .cmd_valid(jstate==J_COMMAND && !read_commanded),
    .cmd_ready(pr_cmd_ready),
    .cmd_addr(work_read_addr),
    .cmd_beats(BEATS),
    .abort(jstate==J_ABORT),
    .busy(pr_busy),
    .done(pr_done),
    .error(pr_error),
    .error_code(pr_code),
    .sticky_fault(pr_fault),
    .m_data(pr_data),
    .m_valid(pr_valid),
    .m_ready(pr_stream_ready),
    .m_last(pr_last),
    .m_axi_arid(pr_arid),
    .m_axi_araddr(pr_araddr),
    .m_axi_arlen(pr_arlen),
    .m_axi_arsize(pr_arsize),
    .m_axi_arburst(pr_arburst),
    .m_axi_arvalid(pr_arvalid),
    .m_axi_arready(pr_arready),
    .m_axi_rid(pr_rid),
    .m_axi_rdata(pr_rdata),
    .m_axi_rresp(pr_rresp),
    .m_axi_rlast(pr_rlast),
    .m_axi_rvalid(pr_rvalid),
    .m_axi_rready(pr_rready)
);

style_axi_read_dma #(.ID_WIDTH(ID_WIDTH),.AXI_ID(3),.BURST_BEATS(READ_BURST_BEATS)) dr_dma(
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .cmd_valid(dstate==D_COMMAND && !refresh_sync[1]),
    .cmd_ready(dr_cmd_ready),
    .cmd_addr((display_half?display_result_addr:display_input_addr)+display_row*WIDTH*4),
    .cmd_beats(WIDTH/4),
    .abort(dstate==D_DRAIN),
    .busy(dr_busy),
    .done(dr_done),
    .error(dr_error),
    .error_code(dr_code),
    .sticky_fault(dr_fault),
    .m_data(dr_data),
    .m_valid(dr_valid),
    .m_ready(dr_stream_ready),
    .m_last(dr_last),
    .m_axi_arid(dr_arid),
    .m_axi_araddr(dr_araddr),
    .m_axi_arlen(dr_arlen),
    .m_axi_arsize(dr_arsize),
    .m_axi_arburst(dr_arburst),
    .m_axi_arvalid(dr_arvalid),
    .m_axi_arready(dr_arready),
    .m_axi_rid(dr_rid),
    .m_axi_rdata(dr_rdata),
    .m_axi_rresp(dr_rresp),
    .m_axi_rlast(dr_rlast),
    .m_axi_rvalid(dr_rvalid),
    .m_axi_rready(dr_rready)
);

style_axi_arbiter #(.ID_WIDTH(ID_WIDTH)) arbiter(
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .s_axi_awid({pw_awid,cw_awid}),
    .s_axi_awaddr({pw_awaddr,cw_awaddr}),
    .s_axi_awlen({pw_awlen,cw_awlen}),
    .s_axi_awsize({pw_awsize,cw_awsize}),
    .s_axi_awburst({pw_awburst,cw_awburst}),
    .s_axi_awvalid({pw_awvalid,cw_awvalid}),

    .s_axi_rready({dr_rready,pr_rready}),
    .m_axi_awid(m_axi_awid),

    .m_axi_awready(m_axi_awready),
    .m_axi_wdata(m_axi_wdata),
    .m_axi_wstrb(m_axi_wstrb),
    .m_axi_wlast(m_axi_wlast),
    .m_axi_wvalid(m_axi_wvalid),
    .m_axi_wready(m_axi_wready),
    .m_axi_bid(m_axi_bid),
    .m_axi_bresp(m_axi_bresp),
    .m_axi_bvalid(m_axi_bvalid),
    .m_axi_bready(m_axi_bready),
    .m_axi_arid(m_axi_arid),
    .m_axi_araddr(m_axi_araddr),
    .m_axi_arlen(m_axi_arlen),
    .m_axi_arsize(m_axi_arsize),
    .m_axi_arburst(m_axi_arburst),
    .m_axi_arvalid(m_axi_arvalid),
    .m_axi_arready(m_axi_arready),
    .m_axi_rid(m_axi_rid),
    .m_axi_rdata(m_axi_rdata),
    .m_axi_rresp(m_axi_rresp),
    .m_axi_rlast(m_axi_rlast),
    .m_axi_rvalid(m_axi_rvalid),
    .m_axi_rready(m_axi_rready)
);

APB 控制与统计寄存器 style_control_regs控制

为未来Sapphire软件提供平台启停、模式、事件、中断和FPS接口，独立模式使用复位默认配置。

时钟域：核心 DDR 时钟域。复位极性以实际端口及下方源码为准。

实现逻辑

APB3访问阶段psel&&penable读写。CONTROL管理enable/auto/bypass；START产生单拍触发，平台保存一个待执行手动任务，重复触发合并。IRQ_STATUS按event_set置sticky，W1C清除，事件同拍发生时置位优先。每个fps_tick计算完整采集和完整处理计数差，饱和到65535。

握手与时序

PREADY固定1；未定义地址或写只读寄存器PSLVERR。paddr是byte offset，真实SoC寄存器基址尚未分配。IRQ为irq_status&irq_enable非零的电平。

状态与流程

寄存器状态、irq_status/irq_enable、前一秒计数；没有隐含软件CPU。

错误处理与恢复

复位默认enable/auto/bypass=1，IRQ使能=0；软件应读IRQ原因、完成处理、写IRQ_CLEAR再进行PLIC应答。配置不能替代DMA/frame安全完成。

实际参数

参数源码默认值
WIDTH640
HEIGHT480

全部实际端口

端口方向位宽声明用途与约定
clkinput1本模块核心/摄像头工作时钟，所属域见上文
rst_ninput1本域低有效复位
paddrinput[11:0]APB3字节offset，12位
pselinput1APB选中从设备
penableinput1APB访问阶段使能
pwriteinput1APB写访问标志
pwdatainput[31:0]APB写32位数据
prdataoutput[31:0]APB读32位数据
preadyoutput1APB响应就绪，当前固定1
pslverroutput1当前APB非法访问错误
enableoutput1允许平台启动新采集和任务
auto_runoutput1自动处理READY输入槽
bypassoutput1请求后续任务使用COPY路径
display_modeoutput[1:0]左右/仅左/仅右显示模式
start_pulseoutput1手动启动一项任务的单拍事件
statusinput[31:0]平台状态位，详见APB表
camera_framesinput[31:0]摄像头输入SOF总计
captured_countinput[31:0]整帧DDR采集成功计数
processed_countinput[31:0]整帧结果DDR成功计数
displayed_countinput[31:0]新前台pair发布计数
drop_countinput[31:0]各阶段丢弃事件合计，可能重复记录同一坏帧
error_countinput[31:0]各阶段错误事件合计，非去重坏帧数
underflow_countinput[31:0]已同步到核心域的显示欠载计数，供APB只读诊断
front_idinput[31:0]当前前台源帧ID
ready_maskinput[3:0]四输入槽处于READY的位掩码
event_setinput[3:0]低4位采集/处理/发布/错误事件
irqoutput1使能sticky事件的中断电平
capture_fpsoutput[15:0]按完整采集计数得到的FPS
process_fpsoutput[15:0]按完整任务计数得到的FPS；当前为COPY
fps_tickinput1统计一秒窗口结束事件

源文件：rtl/style_control_regs.sv；下方内嵌当前源码，可直接在网页搜索。

展开完整源码
// APB3 slave for the future Sapphire SoC. Addresses are offsets, NOT a guessed
// CPU address map. Local standalone mode uses reset defaults (enable/auto/copy).
module style_control_regs #(
    parameter integer WIDTH=640,HEIGHT=480
)(
    input wire clk,rst_n,
    input wire [11:0] paddr,input wire psel,penable,pwrite,
    input wire [31:0] pwdata,output reg [31:0] prdata,
    output wire pready,output reg pslverr,
    output reg enable,auto_run,bypass,
    output reg [1:0] display_mode,output reg start_pulse,
    input wire [31:0] status,camera_frames,captured_count,processed_count,
    input wire [31:0] displayed_count,drop_count,error_count,underflow_count,
    input wire [31:0] front_id,input wire [3:0] ready_mask,
    input wire [3:0] event_set,output wire irq,
    output reg [15:0] capture_fps,process_fps,
    input wire fps_tick
);
    reg [3:0] irq_status,irq_enable;
    reg [31:0] previous_capture,previous_process;
    wire access=psel && penable;
    wire [3:0] irq_clear=(access && pwrite && paddr==12'h018)?pwdata[3:0]:4'b0;
    assign pready=1'b1;
    assign irq=|(irq_status & irq_enable);
    always @* begin
        prdata=0;pslverr=0;
        case(paddr)
            12'h000:prdata=32'h53565931; // SVY1
            12'h004:prdata={29'b0,bypass,auto_run,enable};
            12'h008:prdata=0;
            12'h00c:prdata=status;
            12'h010:prdata={28'b0,irq_status};
            12'h014:prdata={28'b0,irq_enable};
            12'h018:prdata=0;
            12'h020:prdata={30'b0,display_mode};
            12'h030:prdata=WIDTH;
            12'h034:prdata=HEIGHT;
            12'h038:prdata=WIDTH*4;
            12'h03c:prdata=32'h00000001; // RGBX8888 LE word0x00RRGGBB
            12'h040:prdata=32'h01000000;
            12'h044:prdata=32'h00200000;
            12'h048:prdata=32'h00000204; // 4 input slots, 2 result slots
            12'h050:prdata={28'b0,ready_mask};
            12'h054:prdata=front_id;
            12'h060:prdata=camera_frames;
            12'h064:prdata=captured_count;
            12'h068:prdata=processed_count;
            12'h06c:prdata=displayed_count;
            12'h070:prdata=drop_count;
            12'h074:prdata=error_count;
            12'h078:prdata=underflow_count;
            12'h080:prdata={process_fps,capture_fps};
            default:begin prdata=0;pslverr=access;end
        endcase
        if(access && pwrite) case(paddr)
            12'h004,12'h008,12'h014,12'h018,12'h020:;
            default:pslverr=1'b1;
        endcase
    end
    always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
        if(!rst_n) begin
            enable<=1;auto_run<=1;bypass<=1;display_mode<=0;start_pulse<=0;
            irq_status<=0;irq_enable<=0;previous_capture<=0;previous_process<=0;
            capture_fps<=0;process_fps<=0;
        end else begin
            start_pulse<=0;
            irq_status<=(irq_status & ~irq_clear)|event_set; // event wins W1C
            if(access && pwrite && !pslverr) case(paddr)
                12'h004:begin enable<=pwdata[0];auto_run<=pwdata[1];bypass<=pwdata[2];end
                12'h008:start_pulse<=pwdata[0];
                12'h014:irq_enable<=pwdata[3:0];
                12'h020:display_mode<=pwdata[1:0];
                default:;
            endcase
            if(fps_tick) begin
                capture_fps<=(captured_count-previous_capture)>65535?16'hffff:captured_count-previous_capture;
                process_fps<=(processed_count-previous_process)>65535?16'hffff:processed_count-previous_process;
                previous_capture<=captured_count;previous_process<=processed_count;
            end
        end
    end
endmodule
布局与像素显示端 style_display_sink显示

把每行原图再结果的 1280×480 token 流放到 720p 显示的 y120..599。

时钟域：核心域 → 74.25 MHz 像素域。复位极性以实际端口及下方源码为准。

实现逻辑

4096 像素 RAM FIFO 加预取；模式 0 两侧、1 仅左、2 仅右。隐藏的半边仍消耗其全部 tokens。配置在核心域注册快照，再通过内部 ACK 两级同步，在安全窗口锁存。

握手与时序

refresh_request 从启动及每次图像区结束保持到安全 ACK；core_flush 扩展为至少 16 个核心周期共同复位 FIFO；ACK 只表示刷新配置接纳，不等同于 DDR 吞吐保证。

状态与流程

refresh_request、front 配置快照、frame_poisoned；safe_window 默认 y600..749 和 0..99，y100..119 留 guard。

错误处理与恢复

空前台黑屏不报错。有效前台第一次没有 FIFO head 即标粉 FF00FF，余下本帧全粉并停止消费，underflow_count 只增一次。核心同步看到欠载计数变化立即取消并排空旧显示读任务；FIFOflush、槽释放和新配置ACK仍等安全窗口，不能在图像中途重置FIFO。

实际参数

参数源码默认值
H_ACTIVE1280
H_FRONT110
H_SYNC40
H_BACK220
V_ACTIVE720
V_FRONT5
V_SYNC5
V_BACK20
IMAGE_WIDTH640
IMAGE_HEIGHT480
IMAGE_Y120
SAFE_END100
FIFO_ADDR_BITS12
ENABLE_OSD1

全部实际端口

端口方向位宽声明用途与约定
core_clkinput1DDR平台核心时钟
core_rst_ninput1核心域低有效复位
pixel_clkinput1显示像素时钟，默认74.25MHz
pixel_rst_ninput1像素域低有效复位
core_flushinput1共同清空显示FIFO，延长到至少16核心周期
core_front_validinput1本次刷新是否有完整前台pair
core_frame_idinput[31:0]本次刷新原画/结果关联的源帧ID
core_modeinput[1:0]本次刷新显示模式，随ACK锁存
capture_fpsinput[15:0]按完整采集计数得到的FPS
process_fpsinput[15:0]按完整任务计数得到的FPS；当前为COPY
core_camera_framesinput[31:0]核心域RAW SOF累计数，经配置bundle锁存到像素域
core_drop_countinput[31:0]核心域阶段丢弃事件合计，经bundle锁存，非唯一丢帧数
core_error_countinput[31:0]核心域阶段错误事件合计，经bundle锁存，可能重复同一坏帧
bypass_activeinput1当前前台是否由COPY占位生成
s_rgbinput[23:0]输入流：RGB888像素
s_validinput1输入流：有效，握手才推进
s_readyoutput1输入流：接收就绪
refresh_requestoutput1像素域持续刷新请求，需核心两级同步
refresh_ackinput1核心域持续ACK，内部注册快照后跨像素域
safe_windowoutput1像素域安全更新窗口，需核心两级同步
video_rgboutput[23:0]与video_de/hs/vs同拍的RGB888
video_deoutput1有效显示区使能
video_hsoutput1正极性水平同步
video_vsoutput1正极性垂直同步
video_xoutput[15:0]水平扫描坐标，含消隐
video_youtput[15:0]垂直扫描坐标，含消隐
underflow_countoutput[31:0]有效图像FIFO欠载次数，像素域计数需跨域
monitor_fifo_leveloutput[FIFO_ADDR_BITS:0]本模块配置/状态，见实现逻辑及当前源码

源文件：rtl/style_display_sink.sv；下方内嵌当前源码，可直接在网页搜索。

展开完整源码
// Input tokens contain 1280*480 RGB888 pixels: each line is original then
// result, without blanking tokens. A disabled pane still consumes its tokens.
module style_display_sink #(
    parameter integer H_ACTIVE=1280, H_FRONT=110, H_SYNC=40, H_BACK=220,
    parameter integer V_ACTIVE=720, V_FRONT=5, V_SYNC=5, V_BACK=20,
    parameter integer IMAGE_WIDTH=640, IMAGE_HEIGHT=480, IMAGE_Y=120,
    parameter integer SAFE_END=100, FIFO_ADDR_BITS=12, ENABLE_OSD=1
)(
    input wire core_clk, core_rst_n, pixel_clk, pixel_rst_n,
    input wire core_flush, core_front_valid,
    input wire [31:0] core_frame_id,
    input wire [1:0] core_mode,
    input wire [15:0] capture_fps, process_fps,
    input wire [31:0] core_camera_frames, core_drop_count, core_error_count,
    input wire bypass_active,
    input wire [23:0] s_rgb,
    input wire s_valid,
    output wire s_ready,
    output reg refresh_request,
    input wire refresh_ack,
    output wire safe_window,
    output reg [23:0] video_rgb,
    output wire video_de, video_hs, video_vs,
    output wire [15:0] video_x, video_y,
    output reg [31:0] underflow_count,
    // Read-only write-domain FIFO occupancy for passive diagnostics.
    output wire [FIFO_ADDR_BITS:0] monitor_fifo_level
);
    localparam integer IMAGE_END=IMAGE_Y+IMAGE_HEIGHT;
    wire frame_start;
    style_video_timing #(
        .H_ACTIVE(H_ACTIVE),.H_FRONT(H_FRONT),.H_SYNC(H_SYNC),.H_BACK(H_BACK),
        .V_ACTIVE(V_ACTIVE),.V_FRONT(V_FRONT),.V_SYNC(V_SYNC),.V_BACK(V_BACK),
        .SAFE_START(IMAGE_END),.SAFE_END(SAFE_END)
    ) timing (
        .pixel_clk(pixel_clk),.pixel_rst_n(pixel_rst_n),.x(video_x),.y(video_y),
        .de(video_de),.hs(video_hs),.vs(video_vs),.frame_start(frame_start),.safe_window(safe_window)
    );

    // A one-cycle core_flush is expanded to 16 core clocks. FIFO synchronizes
    // deassertion independently in both domains; both pointers reset together.
    reg [4:0] flush_count;
    always @(posedge core_clk or negedge core_rst_n) begin
        if (!core_rst_n) flush_count<=16;
        else if (core_flush) flush_count<=16;
        else if (flush_count!=0) flush_count<=flush_count-1'b1;
    end
    wire flush_active=core_flush || flush_count!=0;
    wire fifo_rst_n=core_rst_n && pixel_rst_n && !flush_active;
    wire [23:0] fifo_rgb;
    wire fifo_valid, fifo_ready;
    wire [FIFO_ADDR_BITS:0] wr_level, rd_level;
    assign monitor_fifo_level=wr_level;
    style_async_fifo #(.WIDTH(24),.ADDR_BITS(FIFO_ADDR_BITS)) fifo (
        .wr_clk(core_clk),.rd_clk(pixel_clk),.async_rst_n(fifo_rst_n),
        .s_data(s_rgb),.s_valid(s_valid),.s_ready(s_ready),
        .m_data(fifo_rgb),.m_valid(fifo_valid),.m_ready(fifo_ready),
        .wr_level(wr_level),.rd_level(rd_level)
    );

    // Bundled-data handshake: snapshot the configuration when core asserts
    // ACK. The snapshot cannot change again until the next request/ACK cycle.
    // Two ACK synchronizer stages then qualify its sampling in pixel domain.
    reg ack_last, bundle_ack;
    reg cfg_valid, cfg_bypass;
    reg [31:0] cfg_frame;
    reg [1:0] cfg_mode;
    reg [15:0] cfg_capture_fps, cfg_process_fps;
    reg [31:0] cfg_camera_frames, cfg_drops, cfg_errors;
    always @(posedge core_clk or negedge core_rst_n) begin
        if (!core_rst_n) begin
            ack_last<=0; bundle_ack<=0; cfg_valid<=0; cfg_bypass<=0; cfg_frame<=0; cfg_mode<=0;
            cfg_capture_fps<=0; cfg_process_fps<=0;
            cfg_camera_frames<=0; cfg_drops<=0; cfg_errors<=0;
        end else begin
            ack_last<=refresh_ack;
            // Register the notification alongside the snapshot. Synchronizing
            // raw ACK directly could outrun snapshot capture with slow core_clk.
            bundle_ack<=refresh_ack;
            if (refresh_ack && !ack_last) begin
                cfg_valid<=core_front_valid; cfg_frame<=core_frame_id; cfg_mode<=core_mode;
                cfg_capture_fps<=capture_fps; cfg_process_fps<=process_fps; cfg_bypass<=bypass_active;
                cfg_camera_frames<=core_camera_frames; cfg_drops<=core_drop_count; cfg_errors<=core_error_count;
            end
        end
    end
    (* async_reg="true" *) reg ack_p1, ack_p2, flush_p1, flush_p2;
    always @(posedge pixel_clk or negedge pixel_rst_n) begin
        if (!pixel_rst_n) begin ack_p1<=0; ack_p2<=0; flush_p1<=1; flush_p2<=1; end
        else begin
            ack_p1<=bundle_ack; ack_p2<=ack_p1;
            flush_p1<=flush_active; flush_p2<=flush_p1;
        end
    end
    reg front_valid, front_bypass;
    reg [31:0] front_frame;
    reg [1:0] front_mode;
    reg [15:0] front_capture_fps, front_process_fps;
    reg [31:0] front_camera_frames, front_drops, front_errors, front_underflows;
    reg frame_poisoned;
    wire image_area=video_de && video_x<2*IMAGE_WIDTH && video_y>=IMAGE_Y && video_y<IMAGE_END;
    wire refresh_at_end=(video_x==0 && video_y==IMAGE_END);
    wire accept_refresh=refresh_request && ack_p2 && safe_window;
    assign fifo_ready=image_area && front_valid && !frame_poisoned && !flush_p2 && !refresh_request;
    always @(posedge pixel_clk or negedge pixel_rst_n) begin
        if (!pixel_rst_n) begin
            refresh_request<=1; front_valid<=0; front_frame<=0; front_mode<=0;
            front_capture_fps<=0; front_process_fps<=0; front_bypass<=0;
            front_camera_frames<=0; front_drops<=0; front_errors<=0; front_underflows<=0;
            frame_poisoned<=0; underflow_count<=0;
        end else begin
            if (refresh_at_end) refresh_request<=1;
            if (accept_refresh) begin
                refresh_request<=0;
                front_valid<=cfg_valid; front_frame<=cfg_frame; front_mode<=cfg_mode;
                front_capture_fps<=cfg_capture_fps; front_process_fps<=cfg_process_fps;
                front_bypass<=cfg_bypass; frame_poisoned<=0;
                front_camera_frames<=cfg_camera_frames; front_drops<=cfg_drops; front_errors<=cfg_errors;
                front_underflows<=underflow_count;
            end
            if (flush_p2) frame_poisoned<=0;
            else if (image_area && front_valid && !frame_poisoned &&
                     !refresh_request && !fifo_valid) begin
                frame_poisoned<=1;
                underflow_count<=underflow_count+1'b1;
            end
        end
    end
    wire osd_hit;
    wire [23:0] osd_rgb;
    style_osd #(.IMAGE_WIDTH(IMAGE_WIDTH),.IMAGE_Y(IMAGE_Y),.IMAGE_HEIGHT(IMAGE_HEIGHT)) osd (
        .x(video_x),.y(video_y),.capture_fps(front_capture_fps),.process_fps(front_process_fps),
        .frame_id(front_frame),.bypass_active(front_bypass),.hit(osd_hit),.rgb(osd_rgb),
        .camera_frames(front_camera_frames),.drop_count(front_drops),.error_count(front_errors),.underflow_count(front_underflows)
    );
    always @* begin
        video_rgb=0;
        if (video_de) begin
            if (image_area && front_valid && !flush_p2 && !refresh_request) begin
                if (frame_poisoned || !fifo_valid) video_rgb=24'hFF00FF;
                else if ((front_mode==1 && video_x>=IMAGE_WIDTH) ||
                         (front_mode==2 && video_x<IMAGE_WIDTH)) video_rgb=0;
                else video_rgb=fifo_rgb;
            end else if (!image_area && ENABLE_OSD && osd_hit) video_rgb=osd_rgb;
        end
    end
endmodule
标准 720p60 时序 style_video_timing显示

生成显示坐标、DE、正极性 HS/VS、帧起点、安全窗口。

时钟域：74.25 MHz 像素时钟域。复位极性以实际端口及下方源码为准。

实现逻辑

默认 H=1280+110+40+220=1650，V=720+5+5+20=750；74.25 MHz 总计对应 60 Hz。有效区原点 x0/y0；HS1390..1429，VS725..729。

握手与时序

持续扫描，不能给显示器施加 ready 背压。RGB 与时序必须保持同拍；新增寄存级须一起延迟。

状态与流程

h_count/v_count 两级扫描计数。

错误处理与恢复

pixel_rst_n 清计数并屏蔽 DE/HS/VS；恢复后从左上角重新扫描。

实际参数

参数源码默认值
H_ACTIVE1280
H_FRONT110
H_SYNC40
H_BACK220
V_ACTIVE720
V_FRONT5
V_SYNC5
V_BACK20
SAFE_START600
SAFE_END100

全部实际端口

端口方向位宽声明用途与约定
pixel_clkinput1显示像素时钟，默认74.25MHz
pixel_rst_ninput1像素域低有效复位
xoutput[15:0]当前水平扫描坐标
youtput[15:0]当前垂直扫描坐标
deoutput1有效显示像素使能
hsoutput1正极性水平同步
vsoutput1正极性垂直同步
frame_startoutput1扫描x0/y0的一拍指示
safe_windowoutput1像素域安全更新窗口，需核心两级同步

源文件：rtl/style_video_timing.sv；下方内嵌当前源码，可直接在网页搜索。

展开完整源码
// Coordinates are active-area based: (0,0) is the first visible pixel.
// Default 74.25 MHz / (1650*750) = 60 Hz, positive sync polarity.
module style_video_timing #(
    parameter integer H_ACTIVE=1280, H_FRONT=110, H_SYNC=40, H_BACK=220,
    parameter integer V_ACTIVE=720, V_FRONT=5, V_SYNC=5, V_BACK=20,
    parameter integer SAFE_START=600, SAFE_END=100
)(
    input wire pixel_clk, pixel_rst_n,
    output wire [15:0] x, y,
    output wire de, hs, vs, frame_start, safe_window
);
    localparam integer H_TOTAL=H_ACTIVE+H_FRONT+H_SYNC+H_BACK;
    localparam integer V_TOTAL=V_ACTIVE+V_FRONT+V_SYNC+V_BACK;
    reg [15:0] h_count, v_count;
    always @(posedge pixel_clk or negedge pixel_rst_n) begin
        if (!pixel_rst_n) begin h_count<=0; v_count<=0; end
        else if (h_count==H_TOTAL-1) begin
            h_count<=0;
            if (v_count==V_TOTAL-1) v_count<=0;
            else v_count<=v_count+1'b1;
        end else h_count<=h_count+1'b1;
    end
    assign x=h_count;
    assign y=v_count;
    assign de=pixel_rst_n && h_count<H_ACTIVE && v_count<V_ACTIVE;
    assign hs=pixel_rst_n && h_count>=H_ACTIVE+H_FRONT && h_count<H_ACTIVE+H_FRONT+H_SYNC;
    assign vs=pixel_rst_n && v_count>=V_ACTIVE+V_FRONT && v_count<V_ACTIVE+V_FRONT+V_SYNC;
    assign frame_start=pixel_rst_n && h_count==0 && v_count==0;
    assign safe_window=pixel_rst_n && (v_count>=SAFE_START || v_count<SAFE_END);
endmodule
文字和诊断叠加 style_osd显示

显示ORIGINAL/RESULT、CAP FPS、COPY FPS、frame_id、RAW/DROP/ERR/UF累计诊断及BYPASS COPY。

时钟域：像素时钟域的组合逻辑。复位极性以实际端口及下方源码为准。

实现逻辑

5×7字模放大两倍，组合生成命中和颜色；FPS十进制四位，frame_id八位十六进制。y88行RAW/DROP/ERR/UF均为八位十六进制累计数，RAW不是FPS。核心统计通过display_sink的同一次bundle握手锁存，UF在像素域accept_refresh锁存。文字不消耗DDR像素。

握手与时序

无流式握手且不额外增加整拍延迟；配置使用 display_sink 锁存的同一组前台值。

状态与流程

字形选择是组合逻辑，无任务状态机。

错误处理与恢复

未接入真正 CNN 前保留 COPY FPS 标签，不能把重复显示或复制吞吐标成推理 FPS。

实际参数

参数源码默认值
IMAGE_WIDTH640
IMAGE_Y120
IMAGE_HEIGHT480

全部实际端口

端口方向位宽声明用途与约定
xinput[15:0]当前水平扫描坐标
yinput[15:0]当前垂直扫描坐标
capture_fpsinput[15:0]按完整采集计数得到的FPS
process_fpsinput[15:0]按完整任务计数得到的FPS；当前为COPY
frame_idinput[31:0]当前前台源帧编号
bypass_activeinput1当前前台是否由COPY占位生成
camera_framesinput[31:0]摄像头输入SOF总计
drop_countinput[31:0]各阶段丢弃事件合计，可能重复记录同一坏帧
error_countinput[31:0]各阶段错误事件合计，非去重坏帧数
underflow_countinput[31:0]像素域刷新时锁存的累计UF数，以十六进制显示，非FPS
hitoutput1当前位置是否命中文字笔画
rgboutput[23:0]命中文字的RGB888

源文件：rtl/style_osd.sv；下方内嵌当前源码，可直接在网页搜索。

展开完整源码
// Combinational 5x7 glyphs, scaled 2x. No timing latency is introduced.
// COPY FPS explicitly describes the placeholder copy path, not CNN inference.
module style_osd #(
    parameter integer IMAGE_WIDTH=640, IMAGE_Y=120, IMAGE_HEIGHT=480
)(
    input wire [15:0] x, y,
    input wire [15:0] capture_fps, process_fps,
    input wire [31:0] frame_id,
    input wire bypass_active,
    input wire [31:0] camera_frames, drop_count, error_count, underflow_count,
    output reg hit,
    output reg [23:0] rgb
);
    function [34:0] glyph;
        input [7:0] c;
        begin
            case(c)
                "A":glyph=35'b01110_10001_10001_11111_10001_10001_10001;
                "B":glyph=35'b11110_10001_10001_11110_10001_10001_11110;
                "C":glyph=35'b01111_10000_10000_10000_10000_10000_01111;
                "D":glyph=35'b11110_10001_10001_10001_10001_10001_11110;
                "E":glyph=35'b11111_10000_10000_11110_10000_10000_11111;
                "F":glyph=35'b11111_10000_10000_11110_10000_10000_10000;
                "G":glyph=35'b01111_10000_10000_10111_10001_10001_01111;
                "I":glyph=35'b11111_00100_00100_00100_00100_00100_11111;
                "L":glyph=35'b10000_10000_10000_10000_10000_10000_11111;
                "M":glyph=35'b10001_11011_10101_10101_10001_10001_10001;
                "N":glyph=35'b10001_11001_11001_10101_10011_10011_10001;
                "O":glyph=35'b01110_10001_10001_10001_10001_10001_01110;
                "P":glyph=35'b11110_10001_10001_11110_10000_10000_10000;
                "R":glyph=35'b11110_10001_10001_11110_10100_10010_10001;
                "S":glyph=35'b01111_10000_10000_01110_00001_00001_11110;
                "T":glyph=35'b11111_00100_00100_00100_00100_00100_00100;
                "U":glyph=35'b10001_10001_10001_10001_10001_10001_01110;
                "V":glyph=35'b10001_10001_10001_10001_10001_01010_00100;
                "W":glyph=35'b10001_10001_10001_10101_10101_10101_01010;
                "Y":glyph=35'b10001_10001_01010_00100_00100_00100_00100;
                "0":glyph=35'b01110_10001_10011_10101_11001_10001_01110;
                "1":glyph=35'b00100_01100_00100_00100_00100_00100_01110;
                "2":glyph=35'b01110_10001_00001_00010_00100_01000_11111;
                "3":glyph=35'b11110_00001_00001_01110_00001_00001_11110;
                "4":glyph=35'b00010_00110_01010_10010_11111_00010_00010;
                "5":glyph=35'b11111_10000_10000_11110_00001_00001_11110;
                "6":glyph=35'b01110_10000_10000_11110_10001_10001_01110;
                "7":glyph=35'b11111_00001_00010_00100_01000_01000_01000;
                "8":glyph=35'b01110_10001_10001_01110_10001_10001_01110;
                "9":glyph=35'b01110_10001_10001_01111_00001_00001_01110;
                ":":glyph=35'b00000_00100_00100_00000_00100_00100_00000;
                default:glyph=0;
            endcase
        end
    endfunction
    function [7:0] hex_char;
        input [3:0] value;
        begin hex_char=value<10 ? 8'd48+value : 8'd65+value-10; end
    endfunction
    function [7:0] decimal_char;
        input [15:0] value;
        input integer digit;
        reg [15:0] limited;
        begin
            limited=value>9999 ? 16'd9999 : value;
            case(digit)
                0:decimal_char=8'd48+(limited/1000)%10;
                1:decimal_char=8'd48+(limited/100)%10;
                2:decimal_char=8'd48+(limited/10)%10;
                default:decimal_char=8'd48+limited%10;
            endcase
        end
    endfunction
    integer local_x, local_y, index, col, row;
    reg [7:0] character;
    reg [34:0] bits;
    always @* begin
        local_x=0; local_y=0; index=0; col=0; row=0;
        character=" "; hit=0; rgb=24'hE0E0E0;
        if (y>=16 && y<30 && x>=8) begin
            local_y=y-16;
            if (x<8+8*12) begin
                local_x=x-8;
                case(local_x/12)
                    0:character="O"; 1:character="R"; 2:character="I"; 3:character="G";
                    4:character="I"; 5:character="N"; 6:character="A"; 7:character="L";
                endcase
                rgb=24'h60E0FF;
            end else if (x>=IMAGE_WIDTH+8 && x<IMAGE_WIDTH+8+6*12) begin
                local_x=x-IMAGE_WIDTH-8;
                case(local_x/12)
                    0:character="R"; 1:character="E"; 2:character="S";
                    3:character="U"; 4:character="L"; 5:character="T";
                endcase
                rgb=24'h80FF80;
            end
        end else if (y>=40 && y<54 && x>=8 && x<8+31*12) begin
            local_x=x-8; local_y=y-40; index=local_x/12;
            case(index)
                0:character="C"; 1:character="A"; 2:character="P";
                4:character="F"; 5:character="P"; 6:character="S"; 7:character=":";
                9,10,11,12:character=decimal_char(capture_fps,index-9);
                15:character="C"; 16:character="O"; 17:character="P"; 18:character="Y";
                20:character="F"; 21:character="P"; 22:character="S"; 23:character=":";
                25,26,27,28:character=decimal_char(process_fps,index-25);
            endcase
        end else if (y>=64 && y<78 && x>=8 && x<8+15*12) begin
            local_x=x-8; local_y=y-64; index=local_x/12;
            case(index)
                0:character="F"; 1:character="R"; 2:character="A";
                3:character="M"; 4:character="E"; 5:character=":";
                7,8,9,10,11,12,13,14:character=hex_char((frame_id >> ((14-index)*4)) & 4'hF);
            endcase
        end else if (y>=88 && y<102 && x>=8 && x<8+61*12) begin
            local_x=x-8; local_y=y-88; index=local_x/12;
            case(index)
                0:character="R"; 1:character="A"; 2:character="W"; 3:character=":";
                5,6,7,8,9,10,11,12:character=hex_char((camera_frames >> ((12-index)*4)) & 4'hF);
                16:character="D"; 17:character="R"; 18:character="O"; 19:character="P"; 20:character=":";
                22,23,24,25,26,27,28,29:character=hex_char((drop_count >> ((29-index)*4)) & 4'hF);
                33:character="E"; 34:character="R"; 35:character="R"; 36:character=":";
                38,39,40,41,42,43,44,45:character=hex_char((error_count >> ((45-index)*4)) & 4'hF);
                49:character="U"; 50:character="F"; 51:character=":";
                53,54,55,56,57,58,59,60:character=hex_char((underflow_count >> ((60-index)*4)) & 4'hF);
            endcase
        end else if (bypass_active && y>=IMAGE_Y+IMAGE_HEIGHT+24 &&
                     y<IMAGE_Y+IMAGE_HEIGHT+38 && x>=8 && x<8+11*12) begin
            local_x=x-8; local_y=y-(IMAGE_Y+IMAGE_HEIGHT+24); index=local_x/12;
            case(index)
                0:character="B"; 1:character="Y"; 2:character="P"; 3:character="A";
                4:character="S"; 5:character="S";
                7:character="C"; 8:character="O"; 9:character="P"; 10:character="Y";
            endcase
            rgb=24'hFFD060;
        end
        col=(local_x%12)/2; row=local_y/2;
        bits=glyph(character);
        if (col<5 && row<7) hit=bits[34-row*5-col];
    end
endmodule
Ti60F225 物理平台顶层 style_ti60_top物理封装

用 Ti60F225 V4 官方平台的 MIPI、DDR、PLL 和高速输出封装接入手写平台核心。

时钟域：摄像头 / DDR 核心 / 像素 / 串化多域。复位极性以实际端口及下方源码为准。

实现逻辑

复制并保留官方 V4/J5 平台必需 IP 和引脚配置，接入重构的预处理、RGB 流 FIFO、平台核心及显示。厂商加密 IP 不解密、不冒充通用 RTL。当前摄像头物理模式保持官方 RAW10 配置；未使用的fabric USER1调试JTAG接口移除，板卡下载JTAG不变。

握手与时序

复位包含核心、DDR、HDMI及摄像头PLL lock。config_done经核心两级同步，与DDR cal_done及abort队列健康共同允许采集DMA。原生CSI VS/HS高有效，显式VS_ACTIVE=1；RAW有效输入为CSI DE&HS。图像/abort经FIFO、计数经Gray跨域。

状态与流程

平台初始化、复位释放和各子模块 FSM，实际顶层连接以嵌入源码为准。

错误处理与恢复

历史经历VS错误无图、半幅紫色、128拍读13fps和短曝光30fps；GBRG2实物失败。COLOR PROBE01版本/参考条正确，右侧BGGR3颜色正确但偏暗，CAP/COPY30、UF/ERR/DROP0。旧正式gamma22显式BGGR3，颜色已获确认但亮度/锐度未验收；gamma18在CDC前，不改曝光/DDR，用户仍反馈灰感；CONTRAST PROBE的标记/KEY1已确认正常，遮镜测得65/66/66/67；BLC版用户确认遮镜纯黑、RIGHT1.00最自然，CAP/COPY30且UF/ERR/DROP0；仍轻微偏绿，白平衡和清晰度未验收；COPY不是CNN，传感器ID没有读回。

实际参数

/////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//
// Copyright (C) 2013-2021 Efinix Inc. All rights reserved.

// Description:
// Example top file for ti60f225 dev kit OOB design
//
// Language:  Verilog 2001
//Ti60f225_sc431hai2hdmi_v6：
//使用了最新版本的framebuffer_v3;
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//`define SOFT_TAP 1
`include "../efinity/vendor/rtl/ddr3_controller/ddr3_parameter.vh"
// `include "define.v"

module style_ti60_top #(

	parameter                       RANK_RATIO         = 1,       // # of unique CS outputs per rank
	parameter                       ASYN_AXI_CLK       = `ASYN_AXI_CLK, 
	parameter                       RANKS              = `RANKS,
	parameter                       CK_WIDTH           = `CK_WIDTH,       // # of CK/CK# outputs to memory   
	parameter                       CKE_WIDTH          = `CKE_WIDTH,       // # of cke outputs
	parameter                       CS_WIDTH           = `CS_WIDTH,       // # of unique CS outputs
	parameter                       BANK_WIDTH         = `BANK_WIDTH,       // # of bank bits
	parameter                       ROW_WIDTH          = `ROW_WIDTH,       // DRAM address bus width
	parameter                       COL_WIDTH          = `COL_WIDTH,      // column address width
	parameter                       DM_WIDTH           = `DM_WIDTH,       // # of DM (data mask)
	parameter                       DQS_WIDTH          = `DQS_WIDTH,       // # of DQS (strobe)
	parameter                       DQ_WIDTH           = `DQ_WIDTH,      // # of DQ (data)
	parameter                       ODT_WIDTH          = `ODT_WIDTH,
	parameter                       DQ_CNT_WIDTH       = `DQ_CNT_WIDTH,       // = ceil(log2(DQ_WIDTH))
	parameter                       DQS_CNT_WIDTH      = `DQS_CNT_WIDTH,       // = ceil(log2(DQS_WIDTH))  
	parameter                       DRAM_WIDTH         = `DRAM_WIDTH,       // # of DQ per DQS   
	parameter                       DATA_WIDTH         = `DATA_WIDTH,
	parameter                       ADDR_WIDTH         = `ADDR_WIDTH,    
	parameter                       AXI_ID_WIDTH       = `AXI_ID_WIDTH,
	parameter                       AXI_ADDR_WIDTH     = `AXI_ADDR_WIDTH,
	parameter                       AXI_DATA_WIDTH     = `AXI_DATA_WIDTH
)
(


   //Clocks 
	input	wire	i_arstn,
    input wire key1_n, key2_n, key3_n,
    input	wire	i_mipi_rx_pclk,
    input wire  vid_clk_dvi2,
    input wire hdmi_tx_slow_clk,
    input wire CLK_5M,
    input  wire CLK_25M,
	output  wire    pll_inst1_RSTN,
	input	wire	i_pll_locked,
	input 	wire  	pll_locked,
	output  wire    pll_inst4_RSTN,
	input   wire    pll_inst4_LOCKED,
	output  wire  	USER_PLL_RSTN,
    output 	wire 	DDR3_PLL_RSTN,
	input 	wire  	user_pll_locked,
    //CSI Interface
    input	 wire		io_cam_sda_IN,
    output wire   io_cam_sda_OUT,
    output wire		io_cam_sda_OE,

    output io_cam_scl_OUT,
    input io_cam_scl_IN,
    output io_cam_scl_OE,

    output	wire		o_cam_rst_p,
    
    input	wire		i_cam_ck_LP_P_IN,
    input	wire		i_cam_ck_LP_N_IN,
    output	wire		o_cam_ck_HS_TERM,
    output	wire		o_cam_ck_HS_ENA,
    input	wire		i_cam_ck_CLKOUT,
    
    input	wire	[7:0]			cam_d0_HS_IN,
    input	  wire		      cam_d0_LP_P_IN,
    input	  wire		      cam_d0_LP_N_IN,
    output	wire		      cam_d0_HS_TERM,
    output	wire		      cam_d0_HS_ENA,
    output	wire		      cam_d0_RST,
    output	wire		      cam_d0_FIFO_RD,
    input	  wire		      cam_d0_FIFO_EMPTY,
    
    input 	wire	[7:0]			cam_d1_HS_IN,
    input	  wire		        cam_d1_LP_P_IN,
    input	  wire		        cam_d1_LP_N_IN,
    output	wire		        cam_d1_HS_TERM,
    output	wire		        cam_d1_HS_ENA,
    output	wire		        cam_d1_RST,
    output	wire		        cam_d1_FIFO_RD,
    input	  wire		        cam_d1_FIFO_EMPTY,
    
    input 	wire	[7:0]			cam_d2_HS_IN,
    input	  wire		        cam_d2_LP_P_IN,
    input	  wire		        cam_d2_LP_N_IN,
    output	wire		        cam_d2_HS_TERM,
    output	wire		        cam_d2_HS_ENA,
    output	wire		        cam_d2_RST,
    output	wire		        cam_d2_FIFO_RD,
    input	  wire		        cam_d2_FIFO_EMPTY,  
   
    input 	wire	[7:0]			cam_d3_HS_IN,
    input	  wire		        cam_d3_LP_P_IN,
    input	  wire		        cam_d3_LP_N_IN,
    output	wire		        cam_d3_HS_TERM,
    output	wire		        cam_d3_HS_ENA,
    output	wire		        cam_d3_RST,
    output	wire		        cam_d3_FIFO_RD,
    input	  wire		        cam_d3_FIFO_EMPTY,

	
	input 					tx_cal_clk_90edge,
  	input 					rx_cal_clk,
  	input 					tx_cal_clk,
	input                              core_clk,     // CORE CLK @ 100MHz
	input                              sdram_clk,    // SDRAM CK @ 400MHz
	// PLL status flags  
	output [2:0]                       pll_shift,  
	output [4:0]                       pll_shift_sel,
	output                             pll_shift_ena,  
	// memory interface ports
	output                             ddr_ck_hi,
	output                             ddr_ck_lo,
	output                             ddr_reset_n,
	output [CKE_WIDTH-1:0]             ddr_cke,     
	output [ROW_WIDTH-1:0]             ddr_addr,
	output [BANK_WIDTH-1:0]            ddr_ba,
	output                             ddr_cas_n,
 
	output [CS_WIDTH*RANK_RATIO-1:0]   ddr_cs_n,
	output                             ddr_ras_n,
	output                             ddr_we_n,
	
	input  [DQS_WIDTH-1:0]             ddr_dqs_in_hi,
	input  [DQS_WIDTH-1:0]             ddr_dqs_in_lo,
	input  [DQ_WIDTH-1:0]              ddr_dq_in_hi,
	input  [DQ_WIDTH-1:0]              ddr_dq_in_lo,
	
	output [DQS_WIDTH-1:0]             ddr_dqs_oe,
	output [DQS_WIDTH-1:0]             ddr_dqs_oe_n,
	output [DQ_WIDTH-1:0]              ddr_dq_oe,  
	output [DQS_WIDTH-1:0]             ddr_dqs_out_hi,
	output [DQS_WIDTH-1:0]             ddr_dqs_out_lo,
	output [DQ_WIDTH-1:0]              ddr_dq_out_hi,
	output [DQ_WIDTH-1:0]              ddr_dq_out_lo,
	output [DM_WIDTH-1:0]              ddr_dm_hi,
	output [DM_WIDTH-1:0]              ddr_dm_lo,
	output [ODT_WIDTH-1:0]             ddr_odt,

       //LED
       output [3:0] led,

       // MIPI DSI
       input	wire	                     i_mipi_tx_pclk		,
       output	wire	                     mipi_dp_clk_LP_P_OUT		,
       output	wire	                     mipi_dp_clk_LP_N_OUT		,
       output	wire	[7:0] 	              mipi_dp_clk_HS_OUT		,
       output	wire	                     mipi_dp_clk_HS_OE		,
       output	wire	                     mipi_dp_data3_LP_P_OUT	,
       output	wire	                     mipi_dp_data2_LP_P_OUT	,
       output	wire	                     mipi_dp_data1_LP_P_OUT	,
       output	wire	                     mipi_dp_data0_LP_P_OUT	,
       output	wire	                     mipi_dp_data3_LP_N_OUT	,
       output	wire	                     mipi_dp_data2_LP_N_OUT	,
       output	wire	                     mipi_dp_data1_LP_N_OUT	,
       output	wire	                     mipi_dp_data0_LP_N_OUT	,
       output	wire	[7:0] 	              mipi_dp_data0_HS_OUT	       ,

       output	wire	                     mipi_dp_clk_RST		,

// from the portable platform core; no Xilinx primitive is used in this project.

// Standalone initializer is retained only until the teammate's I2C driver is
// connected. Future integration must choose ONE I2C owner, never both at once.
wire scl_padoen_o,sda_padoen_o;
reg [12:0] i2c_rst_cnt;
wire i2c_reset_n;
style_reset_sync r_i2c(CLK_5M,w_arstn,i2c_reset_n);
always @(posedge CLK_5M or negedge i2c_reset_n)
    if(!i2c_reset_n)i2c_rst_cnt<=0;
    else if(!i2c_rst_cnt[12])i2c_rst_cnt<=i2c_rst_cnt+1'b1;
wire camera_config_done;
(* async_reg="true" *) reg [1:0] config_done_sync;
always @(posedge core_clk or negedge core_rst_n)
    if(!core_rst_n)config_done_sync<=0;else config_done_sync<={config_done_sync[0],camera_config_done};
style_camera_initializer camera_initializer(.clk(CLK_5M),.rst_n(i2c_rst_cnt[12]),.config_done(camera_config_done),
    .scl_pad_i(io_cam_scl_IN),.scl_pad_o(io_cam_scl_OUT),.scl_padoen_o(scl_padoen_o),
    .sda_pad_i(io_cam_sda_IN),.sda_pad_o(io_cam_sda_OUT),.sda_padoen_o(sda_padoen_o));

wire [9:0] tmds_data0,tmds_data1,tmds_data2,tmds_clk;
// All calibration arithmetic is in the 50 MHz camera domain.
wire [10:0] wb_request_r,wb_request_b,wb_active_r,wb_active_b;
wire [3:0] wb_state;
style_wb_calibrator wb_calibrator(.clk(i_mipi_rx_pclk),.rst_n(camera_rst_n),
    .key_cal_n(key2_n),.key_restore_n(key3_n),.s_vs(w_mipi_rx_vs),
    .mean_valid(cfa_mean_valid),.mean_data(cfa_mean_data),
    .request_r(wb_request_r),.request_b(wb_request_b),
    .active_r(wb_active_r),.active_b(wb_active_b),.state(wb_state));

style_hdmi_display #(
    .RESERVE_CNN(1),.COLOR_PROBE(0),.CONTRAST_PROBE(1),
    .CONTRAST_INITIAL_LEVEL(0),.WB_OVERLAY(1),.PERF_OVERLAY(1)
) hdmi_display(
    .core_clk(core_clk),.core_rst_n(core_rst_n),.camera_clk(i_mipi_rx_pclk),.camera_rst_n(camera_rst_n),
    .pixel_clk(hdmi_tx_slow_clk),.pixel_rst_n(pixel_rst_n),.async_rst_n(w_arstn),
    .core_flush(display_flush),.core_front_valid(front_valid),.core_frame_id(front_id),.core_mode(mode),
    .capture_fps(capture_fps),.process_fps(process_fps),.bypass_active(bypass_active),
    .core_camera_frames(camera_frames_core),.core_drop_count(drop_count),.core_error_count(error_count),
    .s_rgb(display_rgb),.s_valid(display_valid),.s_ready(display_ready),
    .refresh_request(refresh_request),.refresh_ack(display_ack),.safe_window(safe_window),
    .raw_mean_valid(cfa_mean_valid),.raw_mean_data(cfa_mean_data),.key1_n(key1_n),
    .wb_state(wb_state),.wb_active_r(wb_active_r),.wb_active_b(wb_active_b),
    .perf_snapshot(perf_snapshot),.perf_snapshot_valid(perf_snapshot_valid),
    .video_rgb(video_rgb),.video_de(video_de),.video_hs(video_hs),.video_vs(video_vs),.video_x(video_x),.video_y(video_y),
    .tmds_data0(tmds_data0),.tmds_data1(tmds_data1),.tmds_data2(tmds_data2),.tmds_clk(tmds_clk),
    .underflows(underflows),.underflows_core(underflows_core),.fifo_level(display_fifo_level));
assign tmds_tx_clk_TX_DATA=~tmds_clk;
assign tmds_tx_data0_TX_DATA=~tmds_data0;
assign tmds_tx_data1_TX_DATA=~tmds_data1;
assign tmds_tx_data2_TX_DATA=~tmds_data2;
assign tmds_tx_clk_TX_OE=1'b1;assign tmds_tx_data0_TX_OE=1'b1;
assign tmds_tx_data1_TX_OE=1'b1;assign tmds_tx_data2_TX_OE=1'b1;
assign tmds_tx_clk_TX_RST=~pixel_rst_n;assign tmds_tx_data0_TX_RST=~pixel_rst_n;
assign tmds_tx_data1_TX_RST=~pixel_rst_n;assign tmds_tx_data2_TX_RST=~pixel_rst_n;
reg [25:0] heartbeat;
always @(posedge core_clk or negedge core_rst_n)
    if(!core_rst_n)heartbeat<=0;else heartbeat<=heartbeat+1'b1;
assign led={fatal_fault|camera_abort_fault,|error_count,front_valid,cal_done & heartbeat[24]};
// Unused DSI outputs are explicitly disabled; camera uses J5, monitor HDMI TX.
assign LCD_RST_P=1'b0;assign LCD_POWER=1'b0;
assign mipi_dp_clk_LP_P_OUT=0;
assign mipi_dp_clk_LP_N_OUT=0;
assign mipi_dp_clk_HS_OUT=0;
assign mipi_dp_clk_HS_OE=0;
assign mipi_dp_data3_LP_P_OUT=0;
assign mipi_dp_data2_LP_P_OUT=0;
assign mipi_dp_data1_LP_P_OUT=0;
assign mipi_dp_data0_LP_P_OUT=0;
assign mipi_dp_data3_LP_N_OUT=0;
assign mipi_dp_data2_LP_N_OUT=0;
assign mipi_dp_data1_LP_N_OUT=0;
assign mipi_dp_data0_LP_N_OUT=0;
assign mipi_dp_data0_HS_OUT=0;
assign mipi_dp_data1_HS_OUT=0;
assign mipi_dp_data2_HS_OUT=0;
assign mipi_dp_data3_HS_OUT=0;
assign mipi_dp_data3_HS_OE=0;
assign mipi_dp_data2_HS_OE=0;
assign mipi_dp_data1_HS_OE=0;
assign mipi_dp_data0_HS_OE=0;
assign mipi_dp_clk_RST=1'b1;
assign mipi_dp_data0_RST=1'b1;
assign mipi_dp_data1_RST=1'b1;
assign mipi_dp_data2_RST=1'b1;
assign mipi_dp_data3_RST=1'b1;
assign mipi_dp_clk_LP_P_OE=0;
assign mipi_dp_clk_LP_N_OE=0;
assign mipi_dp_data3_LP_P_OE=0;
assign mipi_dp_data3_LP_N_OE=0;
assign mipi_dp_data2_LP_P_OE=0;
assign mipi_dp_data2_LP_N_OE=0;
assign mipi_dp_data1_LP_P_OE=0;
assign mipi_dp_data1_LP_N_OE=0;
assign mipi_dp_data0_LP_P_OE=0;
assign mipi_dp_data0_LP_N_OE=0;
endmodule
按帧性能监测 style_perf_monitor平台监测

被动统计同一发布帧的采集、排队、任务及总耗时，并观察平台AXI流量和FIFO高水位。

时钟域：100MHz核心域。复位极性以实际端口及下方源码为准。

实现逻辑

四个输入槽保存完整32bit帧标签和64bit时间戳；跟随cap/job完成及新pair发布，在发布时整组选择同一帧记录。实板微秒刻度，溢出32位饱和并置标志。AXI按R/W握手计字节、按WSTRB统计写字节，区分地址等待、读数据等待、接收背压、写等待及B响应等待。

握手与时序

只观察事件/AXI，不输出任何数据流控制信号。自动每秒或APB手动拍摄1024bit快照；软件可冻结后读取，自动时核对前后SEQUENCE。PERF_ENABLE默认0，顶层performance-monitor选项显式开启。

状态与流程

每槽tag/capture/job有效位、一个在途任务和一个pending结果标签；读写方向各一个outstanding观察器。

错误处理与恢复

失败帧不给出成功发布耗时；错标签单独累加TAG_MISSES。清统计保留在途时间戳和总线所有权；旧0x000..080寄存器不变。计时不含镜头曝光/前端CDC/显示器延迟，不是纯CNN计算时间。

实际参数

参数源码默认值
CORE_HZ100000000

全部实际端口

端口方向位宽声明用途与约定
clkinput1本模块核心/摄像头工作时钟，所属域见上文
rst_ninput1本域低有效复位
second_tickinput1本模块配置/状态，见实现逻辑及当前源码

cap_doneinput1采集帧槽握手/地址信息：cap_done

wlastinput1本模块配置/状态，见实现逻辑及当前源码
bvalidinput1本模块配置/状态，见实现逻辑及当前源码
breadyinput1本模块配置/状态，见实现逻辑及当前源码
