源文件：rtl/style_perf_monitor.sv；下方内嵌当前源码，可直接在网页搜索。

展开完整源码
// Passive core-domain observer. No output participates in video/AXI flow control.
// Frame timing begins at core capture-slot allocation, NOT sensor exposure.
module style_perf_monitor #(
    parameter integer CORE_HZ=100000000
)(
    input wire clk,rst_n,second_tick,
    input wire cap_start,cap_done,cap_ok,
    input wire [1:0] cap_slot,
    input wire [31:0] cap_id,
    input wire job_start,job_done,job_ok,
    input wire [1:0] job_slot,
    input wire [31:0] job_id,
    input wire publish,
    input wire arvalid,arready,rvalid,rready,rlast,
    input wire awvalid,awready,wvalid,wready,wlast,bvalid,bready,
    input wire [15:0] wstrb,
    input wire [15:0] camera_level,display_level,
    input wire [6:0] paddr,
    input wire psel,penable,pwrite,
    input wire [31:0] pwdata,
    output wire [31:0] prdata,
    output wire pslverr,
    output reg [1023:0] snapshot,
    output reg snapshot_valid
);
    localparam integer DIV=CORE_HZ>=1000000?CORE_HZ/1000000:1;
    localparam integer DW=DIV>1?$clog2(DIV):1;
    localparam integer TICK_HZ=CORE_HZ/DIV;
    reg [DW-1:0] prescale;
    reg [63:0] ticks,cycles,rd_bytes,wr_bytes;
    reg [63:0] previous_cycles,previous_rd,previous_wr;
    reg automatic_snapshot;
    reg [31:0] snapshot_sequence;
    wire access=psel && penable;
    wire control_write=access && pwrite && paddr==0;
    wire clear=control_write && pwdata[2];
    wire take_snapshot=(automatic_snapshot && second_tick) || (control_write && pwdata[1]);
    assign prdata=paddr[6:2]==0?{31'b0,automatic_snapshot}:snapshot[paddr[6:2]*32+:32];
    assign pslverr=access && (paddr[1:0]!=0 || (pwrite && paddr!=0));

    reg [63:0] began[0:3],captured[0:3];
    reg [31:0] tag[0:3],capture_time[0:3],queue_time[0:3],job_time[0:3];
    reg [3:0] tag_valid,capture_valid,job_valid;
    reg working_valid,pending_valid;
    reg [1:0] working_slot,pending_slot;
    reg [31:0] working_id,pending_id;
    reg [63:0] working_began;
    reg [31:0] last_id,last_cap,last_job,last_queue,last_total;
    reg [31:0] max_cap,max_job,max_total,cap_count,job_count,publish_count,tag_misses;
    reg last_valid,latency_overflow;
    wire cap_match=tag_valid[cap_slot] && tag[cap_slot]==cap_id;
    wire start_match=tag_valid[job_slot] && capture_valid[job_slot] && tag[job_slot]==job_id;
    wire job_match=working_valid && working_id==job_id && working_slot==job_slot &&
        tag_valid[job_slot] && tag[job_slot]==job_id;
    wire pub_match=pending_valid && tag_valid[pending_slot] && job_valid[pending_slot] &&
        tag[pending_slot]==pending_id;
    wire [2:0] misses={2'b0,cap_done && cap_ok && !cap_match}+
        {2'b0,job_start && !start_match}+{2'b0,job_done && job_ok && !job_match}+
        {2'b0,publish && !pub_match};
    wire [63:0] cap_elapsed=ticks-began[cap_slot];
    wire [63:0] queue_elapsed=ticks-captured[job_slot];
    wire [63:0] job_elapsed=ticks-working_began;
    wire [63:0] total_elapsed=ticks-began[pending_slot];
    function [31:0] clip32(input [63:0] value);
        clip32=|value[63:32]?32'hffffffff:value[31:0];
    endfunction
    function [31:0] inc(input [31:0] value,input enable);
        inc=(enable && value!=32'hffffffff)?value+1'b1:value;
    endfunction
    // AXI4 is 128-bit, one outstanding transaction per direction in this platform.
    reg read_pending,write_pending,write_last_seen;
    wire ar=arvalid && arready,rr=rvalid && rready;
    wire aw=awvalid && awready,ww=wvalid && wready,br=bvalid && bready;
    wire ar_stall=arvalid && !arready;
    wire read_wait=read_pending && rready && !rvalid;
    wire read_backpressure=rvalid && !rready;
    wire aw_stall=awvalid && !awready;
    wire write_stall=wvalid && !wready;
    wire response_wait=write_pending && write_last_seen && bready && !bvalid;
    reg [31:0] ars,rwait,rbp,aws,wstall,bwait,read_run,write_run,max_read_run,max_write_run;
    reg [15:0] cam_high,display_high;
    reg [4:0] bytes_this_write;
    integer i;
    always @* begin
        bytes_this_write=0;
        for(integer lane=0;lane<16;lane=lane+1) bytes_this_write=bytes_this_write+wstrb[lane];
    end
    always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
        if(!rst_n) begin
            prescale<=0;ticks<=0;cycles<=0;rd_bytes<=0;wr_bytes<=0;
            previous_cycles<=0;previous_rd<=0;previous_wr<=0;
            automatic_snapshot<=1;snapshot_sequence<=0;snapshot<=0;snapshot_valid<=0;
            tag_valid<=0;capture_valid<=0;job_valid<=0;working_valid<=0;pending_valid<=0;
            working_slot<=0;pending_slot<=0;working_id<=0;pending_id<=0;working_began<=0;
            last_id<=0;last_cap<=0;last_job<=0;last_queue<=0;last_total<=0;
            max_cap<=0;max_job<=0;max_total<=0;cap_count<=0;job_count<=0;publish_count<=0;
            tag_misses<=0;last_valid<=0;latency_overflow<=0;
            read_pending<=0;write_pending<=0;write_last_seen<=0;
            ars<=0;rwait<=0;rbp<=0;aws<=0;wstall<=0;bwait<=0;
            read_run<=0;write_run<=0;max_read_run<=0;max_write_run<=0;cam_high<=0;display_high<=0;
            for(i=0;i<4;i=i+1) begin
                began[i]<=0;captured[i]<=0;tag[i]<=0;capture_time[i]<=0;queue_time[i]<=0;job_time[i]<=0;
            end
        end else begin
            cycles<=cycles+1'b1;snapshot_valid<=0;
            if(prescale==DIV-1) begin prescale<=0;ticks<=ticks+1'b1;end
            else prescale<=prescale+1'b1;
            if(control_write) automatic_snapshot<=pwdata[0];
            if(rr) rd_bytes<=rd_bytes+64'd16;
            if(ww) wr_bytes<=wr_bytes+bytes_this_write;
            if(ar) read_pending<=1;
            if(rr && rlast) read_pending<=0;
            if(aw) write_pending<=1;
            if(ww && wlast) write_last_seen<=1;
            if(br) begin write_pending<=0;write_last_seen<=0;end
            ars<=inc(ars,ar_stall);rwait<=inc(rwait,read_wait);rbp<=inc(rbp,read_backpressure);
            aws<=inc(aws,aw_stall);wstall<=inc(wstall,write_stall);bwait<=inc(bwait,response_wait);
            read_run<=read_wait?inc(read_run,1'b1):0;
            write_run<=write_stall?inc(write_run,1'b1):0;
            if(read_wait && inc(read_run,1'b1)>max_read_run) max_read_run<=inc(read_run,1'b1);
            if(write_stall && inc(write_run,1'b1)>max_write_run) max_write_run<=inc(write_run,1'b1);
            if(camera_level>cam_high) cam_high<=camera_level;
            if(display_level>display_high) display_high<=display_level;
            if(misses!=0) begin
                tag_misses<=tag_misses>32'hffffffff-misses?32'hffffffff:tag_misses+misses;
            end
            if(cap_start) begin
                began[cap_slot]<=ticks;tag[cap_slot]<=cap_id;tag_valid[cap_slot]<=1;
                capture_valid[cap_slot]<=0;job_valid[cap_slot]<=0;
            end
            if(cap_done) begin
                if(cap_ok && cap_match) begin
                    captured[cap_slot]<=ticks;capture_time[cap_slot]<=clip32(cap_elapsed);
                    capture_valid[cap_slot]<=1;cap_count<=inc(cap_count,1'b1);
                    if(clip32(cap_elapsed)>max_cap)max_cap<=clip32(cap_elapsed);
                    if(|cap_elapsed[63:32])latency_overflow<=1;
                end else begin tag_valid[cap_slot]<=0;capture_valid[cap_slot]<=0;end
            end
            if(job_start) begin
                working_valid<=start_match;working_slot<=job_slot;working_id<=job_id;working_began<=ticks;
                if(start_match) begin
                    queue_time[job_slot]<=clip32(queue_elapsed);
                    if(|queue_elapsed[63:32])latency_overflow<=1;
                end
            end
            if(job_done) begin
                working_valid<=0;
                if(job_ok && job_match) begin
                    job_time[job_slot]<=clip32(job_elapsed);job_valid[job_slot]<=1;
                    pending_valid<=1;pending_slot<=job_slot;pending_id<=job_id;
                    job_count<=inc(job_count,1'b1);
                    if(clip32(job_elapsed)>max_job)max_job<=clip32(job_elapsed);
                    if(|job_elapsed[63:32])latency_overflow<=1;
                end else begin tag_valid[job_slot]<=0;job_valid[job_slot]<=0;end
            end
            if(publish) begin
                pending_valid<=0;
                if(pub_match) begin
                    last_id<=pending_id;last_cap<=capture_time[pending_slot];last_job<=job_time[pending_slot];
                    last_queue<=queue_time[pending_slot];last_total<=clip32(total_elapsed);last_valid<=1;
                    publish_count<=inc(publish_count,1'b1);
                    if(clip32(total_elapsed)>max_total)max_total<=clip32(total_elapsed);
                    if(|total_elapsed[63:32])latency_overflow<=1;
                end
            end
            if(take_snapshot) begin
                snapshot_sequence<=snapshot_sequence+1'b1;snapshot_valid<=1;
                snapshot[0*32+:32]<=0;snapshot[1*32+:32]<=snapshot_sequence+1'b1;
                snapshot[2*32+:32]<=TICK_HZ;
                snapshot[3*32+:32]<={29'b0,tag_misses!=0,latency_overflow,last_valid};
                snapshot[4*32+:32]<=last_id;snapshot[5*32+:32]<=last_cap;snapshot[6*32+:32]<=max_cap;
                snapshot[7*32+:32]<=last_job;snapshot[8*32+:32]<=max_job;snapshot[9*32+:32]<=last_queue;
                snapshot[10*32+:32]<=last_total;snapshot[11*32+:32]<=max_total;
                snapshot[12*32+:32]<=cap_count;snapshot[13*32+:32]<=job_count;
                snapshot[14*32+:32]<=publish_count;snapshot[15*32+:32]<=tag_misses;
                snapshot[16*32+:64]<=cycles-previous_cycles;
                snapshot[18*32+:64]<=rd_bytes-previous_rd;
                snapshot[20*32+:64]<=wr_bytes-previous_wr;
                snapshot[22*32+:32]<=ars;snapshot[23*32+:32]<=rwait;snapshot[24*32+:32]<=rbp;
                snapshot[25*32+:32]<=aws;snapshot[26*32+:32]<=wstall;snapshot[27*32+:32]<=bwait;
                snapshot[28*32+:32]<=max_read_run;snapshot[29*32+:32]<=max_write_run;
                snapshot[30*32+:32]<={display_high,cam_high};snapshot[31*32+:32]<=CORE_HZ;
                previous_cycles<=cycles;previous_rd<=rd_bytes;previous_wr<=wr_bytes;
            end
            // Start a new interval with THIS edge's observations; the snapshot
            // above includes observations strictly before this clock edge.
            if(take_snapshot || clear) begin
                ars<={31'b0,ar_stall};rwait<={31'b0,read_wait};rbp<={31'b0,read_backpressure};
                aws<={31'b0,aw_stall};wstall<={31'b0,write_stall};bwait<={31'b0,response_wait};
                read_run<={31'b0,read_wait};write_run<={31'b0,write_stall};
                max_read_run<={31'b0,read_wait};max_write_run<={31'b0,write_stall};
                cam_high<=camera_level;display_high<=display_level;
            end
            if(clear) begin
                previous_cycles<=cycles;previous_rd<=rd_bytes;previous_wr<=wr_bytes;
                max_cap<=0;max_job<=0;max_total<=0;cap_count<=0;job_count<=0;publish_count<=0;tag_misses<=0;
                last_valid<=0;latency_overflow<=0;
                // In-flight frame timestamps and AXI ownership deliberately survive clear.
            end
        end
    end
endmodule
性能快照与十进制显示 style_perf_overlay显示

在y104..117空白区显示PERF 01及四页性能指标，原按键功能保留。

时钟域：核心域→74.25MHz像素域。复位极性以实际端口及下方源码为准。

实现逻辑

从快照抽取608bit显示字段，经请求/应答整组信箱跨域，在显示帧起点锁存；单个逐位BCD转换器用128个像素时钟完成四个32bit值转十进制，在第104行前完成。每120个HDMI帧换页，正常60Hz约2秒。

握手与时序

发送端锁存整组payload并翻转请求，双触发器同步请求后目标域采样并返回应答；应答同步返回前源数据保持不变。拥塞合并尚未发送的快照；只影响诊断刷新，视频数据不经过信箱。两个域需共同复位。

状态与流程

最新快照/在途信箱、请求应答同步、像素域最新/当前帧副本、32步乘4字段BCD转换。

错误处理与恢复

没有完整发布记录时延迟值显示横线；其他页仍显示总线数据。页脚字节/窗口值超32位时显示4294967295，APB保留完整64位；诊断行外RGB透传。

实际参数

无可配置模块参数。

全部实际端口

端口方向位宽声明用途与约定
core_clkinput1DDR平台核心时钟
core_rst_ninput1核心域低有效复位
pixel_clkinput1显示像素时钟，默认74.25MHz
pixel_rst_ninput1像素域低有效复位
async_rst_ninput1FIFO两端共同低有效异步复位输入
snapshotinput[1023:0]本模块配置/状态，见实现逻辑及当前源码
snapshot_validinput1本模块配置/状态，见实现逻辑及当前源码
xinput[15:0]当前水平扫描坐标
yinput[15:0]当前垂直扫描坐标
input_rgbinput[23:0]本模块配置/状态，见实现逻辑及当前源码
rgboutput[23:0]命中文字的RGB888

源文件：rtl/style_perf_overlay.sv；下方内嵌当前源码，可直接在网页搜索。

展开完整源码
// Diagnostic-only overlay. Metrics cross in one acknowledged mailbox tuple; decimal conversion
// uses 128 pixel clocks at frame start and is finished before OSD line104.
module style_perf_overlay(
    input wire core_clk,core_rst_n,pixel_clk,pixel_rst_n,async_rst_n,
    input wire [1023:0] snapshot,
    input wire snapshot_valid,
    input wire [15:0] x,y,
    input wire [23:0] input_rgb,
    output reg [23:0] rgb
);
    function [31:0] limit32(input [63:0] v);
        limit32=|v[63:32]?32'hffffffff:v[31:0];
    endfunction
    wire [607:0] selected={snapshot[32+:32],snapshot[30*32+:32],snapshot[29*32+:32],snapshot[28*32+:32],
        snapshot[27*32+:32],snapshot[26*32+:32],snapshot[25*32+:32],snapshot[24*32+:32],
        snapshot[23*32+:32],snapshot[22*32+:32],limit32(snapshot[18*32+:64]),
        limit32(snapshot[20*32+:64]),limit32(snapshot[16*32+:64]),snapshot[10*32+:32],
        snapshot[9*32+:32],snapshot[7*32+:32],snapshot[5*32+:32],snapshot[4*32+:32],snapshot[3*32+:32]};
    // Bundled-data mailbox: the source holds every payload bit unchanged until
    // a two-flop-synchronized acknowledgement returns. The destination samples
    // only after the request passed two flops, then acknowledges that capture.
    wire src_rst_n=core_rst_n && async_rst_n;
    wire dst_rst_n=pixel_rst_n && async_rst_n;
    reg [607:0] newest,mailbox_data;
    reg dirty,request_toggle;
    reg acknowledge_toggle;
    (* async_reg="true" *) reg ack_meta,ack_sync,request_meta,request_sync;
    always @(posedge core_clk or negedge src_rst_n) begin
        if(!src_rst_n) begin ack_meta<=0;ack_sync<=0;end
        else begin ack_meta<=acknowledge_toggle;ack_sync<=ack_meta;end
    end
    always @(posedge pixel_clk or negedge dst_rst_n) begin
        if(!dst_rst_n) begin request_meta<=0;request_sync<=0;end
        else begin request_meta<=request_toggle;request_sync<=request_meta;end
    end
    always @(posedge core_clk or negedge src_rst_n) begin
        if(!src_rst_n) begin newest<=0;mailbox_data<=0;dirty<=0;request_toggle<=0;end
        else begin
            if(request_toggle==ack_sync && dirty) begin
                mailbox_data<=newest;request_toggle<=~request_toggle;dirty<=0;
            end
            if(snapshot_valid) begin newest<=selected;dirty<=1;end
        end
    end
    reg [607:0] latest,shown;
    reg seen,shown_valid,decimal_ready;
    reg [6:0] frames;
    reg [1:0] page,field;
    wire [1:0] next_page=frames==119?page+2'd1:page;
    reg busy;
    reg [5:0] step;
    reg [31:0] binary;
    reg [39:0] bcd,digits[0:3],adjusted;
    wire [39:0] next_bcd={adjusted[38:0],binary[31]};
    integer i;
    function [31:0] metric(input [607:0] v,input [1:0] p,input [1:0] n);
        begin
            case(p)
                0:metric=v[(2+n)*32+:32];
                1:case(n) 0:metric=v[6*32+:32];1:metric=v[8*32+:32];2:metric=v[7*32+:32];default:metric=v[10*32+:32];endcase
                2:case(n) 0:metric=v[9*32+:32];1:metric=v[11*32+:32];2:metric=v[13*32+:32];default:metric=v[14*32+:32];endcase
                default:case(n) 0:metric={16'b0,v[17*32+:16]};1:metric={16'b0,v[17*32+16+:16]};2:metric=v[15*32+:32];default:metric=v[16*32+:32];endcase
            endcase
        end
    endfunction
    always @* begin
        adjusted=bcd;
        for(integer d=0;d<10;d=d+1) if(bcd[d*4+:4]>=5) adjusted[d*4+:4]=bcd[d*4+:4]+4'd3;
    end
    always @(posedge pixel_clk or negedge dst_rst_n) begin
        if(!dst_rst_n) begin
            acknowledge_toggle<=0;latest<=0;shown<=0;seen<=0;shown_valid<=0;decimal_ready<=0;frames<=0;page<=0;
            field<=0;busy<=0;step<=0;binary<=0;bcd<=0;
            for(i=0;i<4;i=i+1)digits[i]<=0;
        end else begin
            if(request_sync!=acknowledge_toggle) begin latest<=mailbox_data;seen<=1;acknowledge_toggle<=request_sync;end
            if(x==0 && y==0) begin
                shown<=latest;shown_valid<=seen;page<=next_page;frames<=frames==119?0:frames+1'b1;
                field<=0;step<=0;bcd<=0;binary<=metric(latest,next_page,0);busy<=1;decimal_ready<=0;
            end else if(busy) begin
                binary<={binary[30:0],1'b0};bcd<=next_bcd;
                if(step==31) begin
                    digits[field]<=next_bcd;step<=0;bcd<=0;
                    if(field==3) begin busy<=0;decimal_ready<=1;end
                    else begin field<=field+1'b1;binary<=metric(shown,page,field+2'd1);end
                end else step<=step+1'b1;
            end
        end
    end
    function [34:0] glyph;
        input [7:0] c;
        begin
            case(c)
                "A":glyph=35'b01110_10001_10001_11111_10001_10001_10001;
                "B":glyph=35'b11110_10001_10001_11110_10001_10001_11110;
                "C":glyph=35'b01111_10000_10000_10000_10000_10000_01111;
                "D":glyph=35'b11110_10001_10001_10001_10001_10001_11110;
                "E":glyph=35'b11111_10000_10000_11110_10000_10000_11111;
                "F":glyph=35'b11111_10000_10000_11110_10000_10000_10000;
                "G":glyph=35'b01111_10000_10000_10111_10001_10001_01111;
                "H":glyph=35'b10001_10001_10001_11111_10001_10001_10001;
                "I":glyph=35'b11111_00100_00100_00100_00100_00100_11111;
                "K":glyph=35'b10001_10010_10100_11000_10100_10010_10001;
                "L":glyph=35'b10000_10000_10000_10000_10000_10000_11111;
                "N":glyph=35'b10001_11001_10101_10011_10001_10001_10001;
                "O":glyph=35'b01110_10001_10001_10001_10001_10001_01110;
                "P":glyph=35'b11110_10001_10001_11110_10000_10000_10000;
                "R":glyph=35'b11110_10001_10001_11110_10100_10010_10001;
                "S":glyph=35'b01111_10000_10000_01110_00001_00001_11110;
                "T":glyph=35'b11111_00100_00100_00100_00100_00100_00100;
                "Y":glyph=35'b10001_10001_01010_00100_00100_00100_00100;
                "0":glyph=35'b01110_10001_10011_10101_11001_10001_01110;
                "1":glyph=35'b00100_01100_00100_00100_00100_00100_01110;
                "2":glyph=35'b01110_10001_00001_00010_00100_01000_11111;
                "3":glyph=35'b11110_00001_00001_01110_00001_00001_11110;
                "4":glyph=35'b00010_00110_01010_10010_11111_00010_00010;
                "5":glyph=35'b11111_10000_10000_11110_00001_00001_11110;
                "6":glyph=35'b01110_10000_10000_11110_10001_10001_01110;
                "7":glyph=35'b11111_00001_00010_00100_01000_01000_01000;
                "8":glyph=35'b01110_10001_10001_01110_10001_10001_01110;
                "9":glyph=35'b01110_10001_10001_01111_00001_00001_01110;
                ":":glyph=35'b00000_00100_00100_00000_00100_00100_00000;
                ".":glyph=35'b00000_00000_00000_00000_00000_00100_00100;
                "-":glyph=35'b00000_00000_00000_11111_00000_00000_00000;
                "J":glyph=35'b00111_00010_00010_00010_10010_10010_01100;
                "M":glyph=35'b10001_11011_10101_10101_10001_10001_10001;
                "U":glyph=35'b10001_10001_10001_10001_10001_10001_01110;
                "W":glyph=35'b10001_10001_10001_10101_10101_11011_10001;
                "Q":glyph=35'b01110_10001_10001_10001_10101_10010_01101;
                "X":glyph=35'b10001_10001_01010_00100_01010_10001_10001;
                default:glyph=0;
            endcase
        end
    endfunction
    function [7:0] hex_char;
        input [3:0] nibble;
        begin hex_char=nibble<10 ? 8'd48+nibble : 8'd65+nibble-10; end
    endfunction


    function [47:0] label(input [1:0] p,input [1:0] n);
        begin case(p)
            0:case(n) 0:label="CAP US";1:label="JOB US";2:label="QUE US";default:label="TOT US";endcase
            1:case(n) 0:label="WIN CY";1:label="RD B  ";2:label="WR B  ";default:label="RWAIT ";endcase
            2:case(n) 0:label="ARWAIT";1:label="RBACK ";2:label="WSTALL";default:label="BWAIT ";endcase
            default:case(n) 0:label="CAM HI";1:label="DIS HI";2:label="RMAXCY";default:label="WMAXCY";endcase
        endcase end
    endfunction
    integer lx,ly,index,pos,col,row,n;
    reg [111:0] heading;
    reg [47:0] name;
    reg [31:0] ident;
    reg [7:0] ch;
    reg [34:0] dots;
    reg [3:0] digit;
    always @* begin
        case(page) 0:heading="PERF 01 LAT F:";1:heading="PERF 01 DDR S:";2:heading="PERF 01 BUS S:";default:heading="PERF 01 MAX S:";endcase
        ident=page==0?shown[32+:32]:shown[18*32+:32];
        rgb=input_rgb;lx=0;ly=0;index=0;pos=0;col=0;row=0;n=0;ch=" ";dots=0;name=0;digit=0;
        if(y>=104 && y<118 && x>=8 && x<8+95*12) begin
            lx=x-8;ly=y-104;index=lx/12;col=(lx%12)/2;row=ly/2;
            if(index<14)ch=heading >> ((13-index)*8);
            else if(index<22)ch=hex_char((ident >> ((21-index)*4)) & 15);
            else if(index>=23)begin
                n=(index-23)/18;pos=(index-23)%18;name=label(page,n[1:0]);
                if(pos<6)ch=name >> ((5-pos)*8);
                else if(pos==6)ch=":";
                else if(pos<17)begin
                    digit=digits[n][(16-pos)*4+:4];
                    ch=shown_valid && decimal_ready && (page!=0 || shown[0])?8'd48+digit:"-";
                end
            end
            dots=glyph(ch);rgb=0;
            if(col<5 && row<7 && dots[34-row*5-col])rgb=24'h80FFFF;
        end
    end
endmodule
按键白平衡校准 style_wb_calibrator采集

KEY2对白纸采样并锁定R/B增益，KEY3恢复固定白平衡。

时钟域：50MHz摄像头域。复位极性以实际端口及下方源码为准。

实现逻辑

按键2FF同步及20ms消抖；等新SOF后采四个完整帧的RAW均值，扣物理黑位并平均。以G为目标，19拍逐位除法依次算R/B的Q8比值，增益范围0.25至4倍。算完后整组请求，由摄像头预处理在SOF锁存。

握手与时序

mean_valid是本域完整帧统计单拍；按键期间旧帧不计。request_r/b为待SOF应用值，active_r/b为已应用值，绿色固定256。状态和已应用增益通过26bit异步FIFO送显示域。

状态与流程

DEFAULT→ARM→SAMPLE(4帧)→CALC→DIV_R→DIV_B→APPLY→LOCKED；失败REJECT，KEY3回默认。

错误处理与恢复

暗分量低于32、任一RAW均值达到960、增益超限或2秒超时，均拒绝并保留原参数。KEY3可取消校准，下个SOF恢复493/504；长按不重复，忙时忽略额外KEY2。当前候选实板待测。

实际参数

参数源码默认值
DEBOUNCE_CYCLES1000000
TIMEOUT_CYCLES100000000
DEFAULT_R493
DEFAULT_B504

全部实际端口

端口方向位宽声明用途与约定
clkinput150MHz摄像头时钟
rst_ninput1低有效复位，恢复固定增益493/504
key_cal_ninput1KEY2低有效，2FF同步和20ms消抖，空闲时触发一次校准
key_restore_ninput1KEY3低有效，优先取消校准并恢复默认
s_vsinput1高有效输入VS，上升沿为生效边界
mean_validinput1本域完整合法帧RAW统计的单拍有效
mean_datainput[39:0]扣黑前A/B/C/D各10bit，A在低位
request_routput[10:0]待下一个SOF锁存的红色Q8增益
request_boutput[10:0]待下一个SOF锁存的蓝色Q8增益
active_routput[10:0]已在SOF生效的红色Q8增益
active_boutput[10:0]已在SOF生效的蓝色Q8增益
stateoutput[3:0]0默认/1等待新帧/2四帧采样/3红除法/4蓝除法/5待生效/6锁定/7拒绝/8算均值

源文件：rtl/style_wb_calibrator.sv；下方内嵌当前源码，可直接在网页搜索。

展开完整源码
// One-shot neutral-target calibration, BGGR / measured black levels only.
// No automatic scene assumptions: the operator must fill the central ROI with white/gray.
module style_wb_calibrator #(
    parameter integer DEBOUNCE_CYCLES=1000000,
    parameter integer TIMEOUT_CYCLES=100000000,
    parameter integer DEFAULT_R=493,
    parameter integer DEFAULT_B=504
)(
    input wire clk, rst_n,
    input wire key_cal_n, key_restore_n,
    input wire s_vs,
    input wire mean_valid,
    input wire [39:0] mean_data,
    output reg [10:0] request_r, request_b,
    output reg [10:0] active_r, active_b,
    // 0 DEFAULT, 1 ARM, 2 SAMPLE, 3/4 DIVIDE, 5 APPLY, 6 LOCKED, 7 REJECT, 8 CALC.
    output reg [3:0] state
);
    localparam integer DW=DEBOUNCE_CYCLES>1?$clog2(DEBOUNCE_CYCLES):1;
    localparam integer TW=TIMEOUT_CYCLES>1?$clog2(TIMEOUT_CYCLES):1;
    (* async_reg="true" *) reg [1:0] key_meta,key_sync;
    reg [1:0] stable_key, press;
    reg [DW-1:0] debounce[0:1];
    reg vs_q, restore_pending;
    wire sof=s_vs && !vs_q;
    integer k;
    always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
        if(!rst_n) begin key_meta<=3;key_sync<=3; end
        else begin key_meta<={key_restore_n,key_cal_n};key_sync<=key_meta;end
    end
    always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
        if(!rst_n) begin stable_key<=3;press<=0;debounce[0]<=0;debounce[1]<=0;end
        else begin
            press<=0;
            for(k=0;k<2;k=k+1) begin
                if(key_sync[k]==stable_key[k]) debounce[k]<=0;
                else if(debounce[k]==DEBOUNCE_CYCLES-1) begin
                    stable_key[k]<=key_sync[k];debounce[k]<=0;
                    if(!key_sync[k]) press[k]<=1;
                end else debounce[k]<=debounce[k]+1'b1;
            end
        end
    end
    wire [9:0] a=mean_data[9:0],b=mean_data[19:10],c=mean_data[29:20],d=mean_data[39:30];
    wire [9:0] r=d>67?d-10'd67:10'd0;
    wire [9:0] bl=a>65?a-10'd65:10'd0;
    wire [9:0] gb=b>66?b-10'd66:10'd0, gc=c>66?c-10'd66:10'd0;
    wire [10:0] gs={1'b0,gb}+{1'b0,gc};
    wire [9:0] g=gs[10:1];
    wire acceptable=r>=32 && bl>=32 && gb>=32 && gc>=32 &&
                    a<960 && b<960 && c<960 && d<960;
    reg [11:0] sum_r,sum_g,sum_b;
    reg [1:0] samples;
    wire [9:0] avg_r=sum_r[11:2],avg_g=sum_g[11:2],avg_b=sum_b[11:2];
    reg [TW-1:0] timeout_count;
    // Restoring division: 19 clocks per ratio, no combinational variable divider.
    reg [18:0] numerator,quotient;
    reg [10:0] remainder;
    reg [9:0] divisor;
    reg [4:0] bit_count;
    reg [10:0] candidate_r;
    wire [11:0] trial={remainder,numerator[18]};
    wire take=trial>={2'b0,divisor};
    wire [18:0] next_q={quotient[17:0],take};
    wire [11:0] next_rem=take?trial-{2'b0,divisor}:trial;
    wire busy=state!=0 && state!=6 && state!=7;
    always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
        if(!rst_n) begin
            vs_q<=0;state<=0;restore_pending<=0;timeout_count<=0;
            request_r<=DEFAULT_R;request_b<=DEFAULT_B;active_r<=DEFAULT_R;active_b<=DEFAULT_B;
            sum_r<=0;sum_g<=0;sum_b<=0;samples<=0;
            numerator<=0;quotient<=0;remainder<=0;divisor<=1;bit_count<=0;candidate_r<=0;
        end else begin
            vs_q<=s_vs;
            if(busy) timeout_count<=timeout_count+1'b1;else timeout_count<=0;
            // Requests and capture latch on the same SOF; report the applied tuple.
            if(sof) begin active_r<=request_r;active_b<=request_b;end
            if(press[1]) begin
                request_r<=DEFAULT_R;request_b<=DEFAULT_B;state<=5;
                restore_pending<=1;timeout_count<=0;
            end else if(press[0] && !busy) begin
                // Ignore extra calibration presses while a request is busy.
                state<=1;restore_pending<=0;timeout_count<=0;
                sum_r<=0;sum_g<=0;sum_b<=0;samples<=0;
            end else if(busy && timeout_count==TIMEOUT_CYCLES-1 && !(state==5 && sof)) begin
                state<=7;request_r<=active_r;request_b<=active_b;
            end else case(state)
                1:if(sof) state<=2; // Ignore the frame already in flight at the key press.
                2:if(mean_valid) begin
                    if(!acceptable) state<=7;
                    else begin
                        sum_r<=sum_r+r;sum_g<=sum_g+g;sum_b<=sum_b+bl;
                        if(samples==3) state<=8;else samples<=samples+1'b1;
                    end
                end
                8:begin
                    numerator<={1'b0,avg_g,8'b0}+{10'b0,avg_r[9:1]};
                    divisor<=avg_r;quotient<=0;remainder<=0;bit_count<=0;state<=3;
                end
                3,4:begin
                    numerator<={numerator[17:0],1'b0};quotient<=next_q;
                    remainder<=next_rem[10:0];bit_count<=bit_count+1'b1;
                    if(bit_count==18) begin
                        if(next_q<64 || next_q>1024) state<=7;
                        else if(state==3) begin
                            candidate_r<=next_q[10:0];
                            numerator<={1'b0,avg_g,8'b0}+{10'b0,avg_b[9:1]};
                            divisor<=avg_b;quotient<=0;remainder<=0;bit_count<=0;state<=4;
                        end else begin
                            request_r<=candidate_r;request_b<=next_q[10:0];state<=5;
                        end
                    end
                end
                5:if(sof) state<=restore_pending?0:6;
                default:;
            endcase
        end
    end
endmodule
白平衡状态显示 style_wb_overlay显示

显示WB CAL 01、校准状态和已应用R/B十六进制Q8增益。

时钟域：74.25MHz HDMI像素域。复位极性以实际端口及下方源码为准。

实现逻辑

y628至641绘制状态页脚；采样时在左画面画中心RAW ROI边框。其他像素透传，不修改DDR数据。

握手与时序

顶层异步FIFO跨域传26bit整组状态，HDMI帧起点再锁存；输入应为本显示帧稳定的数据。

状态与流程

组合字体和区域选择，无状态机。

错误处理与恢复

复位显示DEFAULT及默认增益，REJECT使用橙色文字。状态显示有跨域与帧锁存延迟，不与某个摄像头frame_id严格对应。

实际参数

无可配置模块参数。

全部实际端口

端口方向位宽声明用途与约定
xinput[15:0]HDMI横坐标
yinput[15:0]HDMI纵坐标
input_rgbinput[23:0]对比度叠加后的RGB888
stateinput[3:0]经FIFO跨域并在显示帧起点锁存的校准状态
gain_rinput[10:0]已经生效的红色Q8增益，页脚显示十六进制
gain_binput[10:0]已经生效的蓝色Q8增益，页脚显示十六进制
rgboutput[23:0]新增WB CAL 01状态文字；采样时标出左图中心ROI；不回写DDR

源文件：rtl/style_wb_overlay.sv；下方内嵌当前源码，可直接在网页搜索。

展开完整源码
// Pixel-domain diagnostic only. Inputs must be atomically frame-latched.
module style_wb_overlay(
    input wire [15:0] x,y,
    input wire [23:0] input_rgb,
    input wire [3:0] state,
    input wire [10:0] gain_r,gain_b,
    output reg [23:0] rgb
);
    function [34:0] glyph;
        input [7:0] c;
        begin
            case(c)
                "A":glyph=35'b01110_10001_10001_11111_10001_10001_10001;
                "B":glyph=35'b11110_10001_10001_11110_10001_10001_11110;
                "C":glyph=35'b01111_10000_10000_10000_10000_10000_01111;
                "D":glyph=35'b11110_10001_10001_10001_10001_10001_11110;
                "E":glyph=35'b11111_10000_10000_11110_10000_10000_11111;
                "F":glyph=35'b11111_10000_10000_11110_10000_10000_10000;
                "G":glyph=35'b01111_10000_10000_10111_10001_10001_01111;
                "H":glyph=35'b10001_10001_10001_11111_10001_10001_10001;
                "I":glyph=35'b11111_00100_00100_00100_00100_00100_11111;
                "K":glyph=35'b10001_10010_10100_11000_10100_10010_10001;
                "L":glyph=35'b10000_10000_10000_10000_10000_10000_11111;
                "N":glyph=35'b10001_11001_10101_10011_10001_10001_10001;
                "O":glyph=35'b01110_10001_10001_10001_10001_10001_01110;
                "P":glyph=35'b11110_10001_10001_11110_10000_10000_10000;
                "R":glyph=35'b11110_10001_10001_11110_10100_10010_10001;
                "S":glyph=35'b01111_10000_10000_01110_00001_00001_11110;
                "T":glyph=35'b11111_00100_00100_00100_00100_00100_00100;
                "Y":glyph=35'b10001_10001_01010_00100_00100_00100_00100;
                "0":glyph=35'b01110_10001_10011_10101_11001_10001_01110;
                "1":glyph=35'b00100_01100_00100_00100_00100_00100_01110;
                "2":glyph=35'b01110_10001_00001_00010_00100_01000_11111;
                "3":glyph=35'b11110_00001_00001_01110_00001_00001_11110;
                "4":glyph=35'b00010_00110_01010_10010_11111_00010_00010;
                "5":glyph=35'b11111_10000_10000_11110_00001_00001_11110;
                "6":glyph=35'b01110_10000_10000_11110_10001_10001_01110;
                "7":glyph=35'b11111_00001_00010_00100_01000_01000_01000;
                "8":glyph=35'b01110_10001_10001_01110_10001_10001_01110;
                "9":glyph=35'b01110_10001_10001_01111_00001_00001_01110;
                ":":glyph=35'b00000_00100_00100_00000_00100_00100_00000;
                ".":glyph=35'b00000_00000_00000_00000_00000_00100_00100;
                "-":glyph=35'b00000_00000_00000_11111_00000_00000_00000;
                "J":glyph=35'b00111_00010_00010_00010_10010_10010_01100;
                "M":glyph=35'b10001_11011_10101_10101_10001_10001_10001;
                "U":glyph=35'b10001_10001_10001_10001_10001_10001_01110;
                "W":glyph=35'b10001_10001_10001_10101_10101_11011_10001;
                default:glyph=0;
            endcase
        end
    endfunction
    function [7:0] hex_char;
        input [3:0] nibble;
        begin hex_char=nibble<10 ? 8'd48+nibble : 8'd65+nibble-10; end
    endfunction

    reg [63:0] label;
    reg [7:0] ch;
    reg [34:0] dots;
    reg [87:0] heading;
    reg [159:0] help_text;
    integer lx,ly,index,col,row;
    always @* begin
        case(state)
            0:label="DEFAULT ";
            1,2:label="SAMPLE  ";
            3,4,8:label="CALC    ";
            5:label="APPLY   ";
            6:label="LOCKED  ";
            default:label="REJECT  ";
        endcase
        heading="WB CAL 01  ";help_text="  KEY2 CAL KEY3 RESET";
        rgb=input_rgb;ch=" ";dots=0;lx=0;ly=0;index=0;col=0;row=0;
        if(y>=628 && y<642 && x>=8 && x<8+52*12) begin
            lx=x-8;ly=y-628;index=lx/12;col=(lx%12)/2;row=ly/2;
            if(index<11) ch=heading >> ((10-index)*8);
            else if(index<19) ch=label >> ((18-index)*8);
            else case(index)
                19:ch="R";20:ch=":";
                21:ch=hex_char({1'b0,gain_r[10:8]});
                22:ch=hex_char(gain_r[7:4]);23:ch=hex_char(gain_r[3:0]);
                25:ch="B";26:ch=":";
                27:ch=hex_char({1'b0,gain_b[10:8]});
                28:ch=hex_char(gain_b[7:4]);29:ch=hex_char(gain_b[3:0]);
                default:if(index>=30 && index<50) ch=help_text >> ((49-index)*8);
            endcase
            dots=glyph(ch);
            rgb=24'h000000;
            if(col<5 && row<7 && dots[34-row*5-col])
                rgb=state==7?24'hFF8060:24'hFFFF80;
        end
        // Center RAW ROI outline, display-only, visible while collecting paper.
        if(state==1 || state==2) begin
            if(((x==298 || x==342) && y>=331 && y<=389) ||
               ((y==331 || y==389) && x>=298 && x<=342)) rgb=24'hFFFF00;
        end
    end
endmodule
